\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})(\|\partial_{x}\nabla\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}),

which implies that

\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{4}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{4}_{X_{a}}).

Estimate of
\eqref{eq:nonlinear-neq0}_{4}
.

Similar to
(\ref{appa_1})_{2}
, one can prove

\|\nabla u_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\nabla\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}),

and combining it with
(\ref{appa_2})_{3},
there holds

\displaystyle\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}^{2}\|\nabla u_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}(\|\partial_{x}\nabla\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}),

which implies that
\eqref{eq:nonlinear-neq0}_{4}
.

Estimate of
\eqref{eq:nonlinear-neq0}_{5}
.

Using Lemma
3.2
and Lemma
3.13
again,
we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}u_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y,z}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}^{2}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}).

(3.21)

Combining (
3.21
),
(\ref{appa_2})_{3}
and
(\ref{appa_2})_{4}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}u_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y,z}}^{2}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})(\|\partial_{x}\nabla\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}),

which implies that

\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{4}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{4}_{X_{a}}).

Estimates of
\eqref{eq:nonlinear-neq0}_{6}
and
\eqref{eq:nonlinear-neq0}_{7}
.

For
j=1,3
, using Lemma
3.2
and
3.13
again, there are

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y,z}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}),

(3.22)

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\leq C\|\nabla(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial_{y}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{y}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}})(\|\partial_{y}\nabla\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}),

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\leq C\|\nabla u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla\partial_{y}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\partial_{y}\nabla\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}).

By
(\ref{appa_1})_{2}
,
(\ref{appa_2})_{4}
and (
3.22
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y,z}}\|\nabla u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\|\partial_{y}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle+\|u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\nabla(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}+\|u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial_{y}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})(\|\triangle\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}),

which implies
\eqref{eq:nonlinear-neq0}_{6}
and
\eqref{eq:nonlinear-neq0}_{7}
.

The proof is complete. ∎

Lemma 3.15
.

For
j=2,3
, there hold

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}\nabla u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{1,3}^{2}A^{1-2\epsilon}\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}},

(3.23)

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{1,3}^{2}A^{1-2\epsilon}(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}})\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}},

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{1,3}^{2}A^{1-2\epsilon}(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}})\|\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}},

\displaystyle\|e^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{1,3}^{2}A^{\frac{5}{3}-2\epsilon}\left(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}},

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla u_{1,0}\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{1,3}^{2}A^{\frac{4}{3}-2\epsilon}(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}})\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}},

\displaystyle\|e^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{1,3}^{2}A^{\frac{5}{3}-2\epsilon}\left(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}\right)\|\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}},

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{y},\partial_{z})(u_{2,\neq}\nabla u_{1,0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CE_{1,3}^{2}A^{1-2\epsilon}\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{X_{a}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u_{3,\neq}\nabla u_{1,0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CE_{1,3}^{2}A^{\frac{4}{3}-2\epsilon}(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}})\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}.

Proof.

Estimate of
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{1}
.

First of all, by (
2.3
) we get

\displaystyle\|{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}+\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}\leq\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{s}\widehat{u_{1,0}}(s)\|_{H^{2}}ds+\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}

(3.24)

\displaystyle\leq CE_{1,3}A^{\frac{1}{3}-\epsilon}(1+A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t).

Then

\big\|{\rm e}^{-\frac{a}{4}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\|{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}-\epsilon}E_{1,3}
, since
\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}(1+A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t){\rm e}^{-\frac{a}{4}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}=0.

Combining this with
\|u_{1,0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|u_{1,0}\|_{H^{2}}
, by
Hölder’s inequality we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}\nabla u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{-\frac{a}{2}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}s}\|{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}{\rm e}^{\frac{5a}{2}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}s}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-2\epsilon}E_{1,3}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{1-2\epsilon}E_{1,3}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}.

Estimate of
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{2}
.

Similarly, with the help of Lemma
3.13
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-2\epsilon}E_{1,3}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq CE^{2}_{1,3}A^{1-2\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}).

Estimate of
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{3}
.

It can be proved by using the divergence-free property

\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}=-\left(\partial_{y}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}\right),

(3.25)

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}})\|\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}.

Estimate of
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{4}
.
Using (
3.25
) and Lemma
3.13
, one deduces

\displaystyle\|u_{1,0}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|u_{1,0}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|u_{1,0}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\left(\|\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|u_{1,0}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}\nabla(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}\nabla\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\right),

which gives
(\ref{lemma_neq2_2})_{4}
.

Estimate of
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{5}
.

Using Lemma
3.1
,
(\ref{sob_result_2})_{6}
and Lemma
3.13
, we get

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{1,0}\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\nabla u_{1,0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{1,0}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|u_{1,0}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

which implies
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{5}
.

Estimate of
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{6}
.

Similar to
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{5}
, and using (
3.25
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\|u_{1,0}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|u_{1,0}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}\nabla(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}\nabla\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\right),

which gives
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{6}
.

Estimate of
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{7}
.

Using
(\ref{appa_1})_{1}
,
(\ref{appa_4})_{3}
and Hölder’s inequality, there holds

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{y},\partial_{z})(u_{2,\neq}\nabla u_{1,0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{1,0}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}\|\nabla u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{1,0}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}\|u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}},

which along with
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{1}
indicate that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{y},\partial_{z})(u_{2,\neq}\nabla u_{1,0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\big\|{\rm e}^{-\frac{a}{8}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\|{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq CE_{1,3}^{2}A^{1-2\epsilon}\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Estimate of
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{8}
.

Using Lemma
3.2
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{z}\partial_{y}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{3,\neq}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|^{{}^{\frac{1}{2}}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{3,\neq}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|^{{}^{\frac{1}{2}}}_{L^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\|u_{3,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{y}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{3,\neq}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})u_{3,\neq}\|^{{}^{\frac{1}{2}}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{3,\neq}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})u_{3,\neq}\|^{{}^{\frac{1}{2}}}_{L^{2}}.

Using Lemma
3.13
again, we get

\displaystyle\quad\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u_{3,\neq}\nabla u_{1,0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CE_{1,3}^{2}A^{\frac{4}{3}-2\epsilon}(\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}})\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Lemma 3.16
.

For
j=2,3
, it holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{j,0}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\nabla u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA(\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}),

(3.26)

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}\nabla u_{j,0}\cdot u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}(\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}),

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\nabla u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA(\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}),

\displaystyle\|e^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{y}u_{3,0}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\right)\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}.

Proof.

By Lemma
3.1
and
\partial_{y}u_{2,0}+\partial_{z}u_{3,0}=0
, we have

\displaystyle\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{H^{2}},\quad\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}),

(3.27)

which along with Lemma
3.13
imply that

\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{j,0}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\nabla u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA(\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}).

Moreover,
\eqref{eq:zeroneq0}_{2}
and
\eqref{eq:zeroneq0}_{3}
can be proved by using Lemma
3.13
and

\displaystyle\|u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{y}u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\nabla u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Using Lemma
3.1
,
(\ref{sob_result_2})_{7}
in Lemma
3.2
and
\partial_{z}u_{3,0}=-\partial_{y}u_{2,0}
, we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}u_{3,0}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}L^{\infty}_{z}}^{2}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\partial_{y}u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{y}u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|u_{2,0}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\right)\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

which follows that

\displaystyle\|e^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{y}u_{3,0}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\right)\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}.

The proof is complete.
∎

4.
Estimates for the zero mode: Proof of Proposition
2.1

In this section, we are aimed to estimate the zero modes for both
n
and

u
, where the energy transfer mechanism plays an important role.

Energy transfer mechanism:

To estimate zero modes, we first need to observe and analyze the energy transfer mechanism.

•

Under assumptions (
2.7
), the energy
\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}
will not be affected by any other energy. And we use the equilibrium point analysis method to obtain the precise bound of this energy.

•

The z-part non-zero mode
n_{(0,\neq)}
is affected by
n_{(0,0)}
, as long as
n_{(0,0)}
satisfies certain conditions, we can estimate it under smallness
A^{\epsilon}\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T})}\leq C
.

•

The energy
E_{1,2}
is affected by
E_{1,1}
.
After estimating
E_{1,1}
, it is easy to
estimates
E_{1,2}
under the smallness
A^{\epsilon}\|(u_{\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T})}\leq C.

•

Due to 3D lift-up effect,
E_{1,3}
is affected by
E_{1,2}
,
after obtaining all estimates of
u_{2,0}
and
u_{3,0},
the energy
E_{1,3}
can be obtained directly.

In this way, we can estimate all zero modes in terms of

n_{(0,0)}\rightarrow n_{(0,\neq)}\rightarrow\{u_{2,0},u_{3,0}\}\rightarrow u_{1,0}.

4.1.
Energy estimates for
E_{1,1}(t)

It follows from (
1.5
) that the zero mode
n_{0}
satisfies

\displaystyle\partial_{t}n_{0}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle n_{0}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\left[\nabla\cdot(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{0}+\partial_{y}(n_{0}\partial_{y}c_{0})+\partial_{z}(n_{0}\partial_{z}c_{0})\right]

(4.1)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\left[\nabla\cdot(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{0}+\partial_{y}(u_{2,0}n_{0})+\partial_{z}(u_{3,0}n_{0})\right].

Due to
u_{2,(0,0)}=0
, the z-part zero mode
n_{(0,0)}
follows:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}n_{(0,0)}-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{yy}n_{(0,0)}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\partial_{y}\left(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\right)+\partial_{y}(n_{\neq}\partial_{y}c_{\neq})_{(0,0)}+\partial_{y}(u_{2,\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,0)}

(4.2)

\displaystyle+\partial_{y}\big(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)}\big)_{(0,0)}+\partial_{y}\big(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)}\big)_{(0,0)}\Big).

To estimate
n_{(0,0)}
, we first consider a simplified model of (
4.2
) in the form of

\displaystyle\partial_{t}n_{(0,0)}-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{yy}n_{(0,0)}=-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\left(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\right).

(4.3)

Lemma 4.1
.

For the simplified equation (
4.3
), there holds

\displaystyle\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq\max\left\{\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{32\pi^{2}}\right\}.

It should be noted that
M_{1}=\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}=\frac{1}{|\mathbb{T}|^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}n(t,x,y,z)dxdydz=\frac{M}{|\mathbb{T}|^{2}}.

Proof.

Our proof mainly relies on equilibrium point analysis method in dynamical systems and a priori estimates in Section
3
.

By energy estimates, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{A}\|n_{(0,0)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}.

Using the Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality (
A.1
) and elliptic estimates in Lemma
3.10
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{yy}c_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

which implies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}A}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}

(4.4)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{4}_{L^{2}}}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}A}+\frac{c_{1}}{2\sqrt{2}A}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

where
c_{1}
is a positive constant with
c_{1}\in(0,2\sqrt{2}).

Thanks to the Nash’s inequality (
A.2
) with the sharp constant (see also
[
35
,
42
]
)

\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\left(\frac{16\pi^{2}}{27}\right)^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{2}{3}},

we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\frac{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1})}{27M_{1}^{4}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{6}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{4}_{L^{2}}\Big),

(4.5)

where
M_{1}=\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}.

Introduce the auxiliary function
h(t)=\|n_{(0,0)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}
satisfying
h\geq 0,

then (
4.5
) becomes

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\frac{1}{2}\frac{dh}{dt}\leq-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\frac{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1})}{27M_{1}^{4}}h^{3}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}}h^{2}\Big):=H(h),\quad h\geq 0,\\
\\
h(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.\end{array}\right.

(4.6)

Let
\mathcal{H}(\frac{dh}{dt},h)=\frac{1}{2}\frac{dh}{dt}-H(h),

and the
bound of
\|n_{(0,0)}(t)\|_{L^{2}}
can be obtained by studying the orbits on the phase plane
(\frac{dh}{dt},h)\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}.

The phase portraits are obtained by plotting the level curves of the function
\mathcal{H}(\frac{dh}{dt},h)
,
and the phase portrait in the phase plane
(\frac{dh}{dt},h)\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}
is shown in Figure
1
,
where the black dotted line is
\frac{dh}{dt}=0.

It should be noted that the point where the dashed line intersects with the dotted line is the equilibrium point.

Figure 1.
Phase portrait in the phase plane
(\frac{dh}{dt},h)\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}.

In Figure
1
, the yellow solid line is the orbit for
\mathcal{H}(\frac{dh}{dt},h)=0
corresponding to the case of

\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}=-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\frac{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1})}{27M_{1}^{4}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{6}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{4}_{L^{2}}\Big),

and other solid lines represent
\mathcal{H}(\frac{dh}{dt},h)=c
with

c\in(-\infty,0)
, which corresponding to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\frac{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1})}{27M_{1}^{4}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{6}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{4}_{L^{2}}\Big)+c

\displaystyle<-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\frac{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1})}{27M_{1}^{4}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{6}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{4}_{L^{2}}\Big).

We only need to consider

\mathcal{H}(\frac{dh}{dt},h)=0,
which corresponding to a dynamic system:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\frac{1}{2}\frac{dh}{dt}=-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\frac{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1})}{27M_{1}^{4}}h^{3}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}}h^{2}\Big):=H(h),\quad h\geq 0,\\
\\
h(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.\end{array}\right.

(4.7)

The red cross in Figure
1
is the equilibrium point of system (
4.7
) satisfying

\frac{dh^{*}}{dt}=0,\quad h^{*}=\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{16\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1})c_{1}}.

It is easy to get that
\frac{d}{dh}H(h^{*})<0,
thus this equilibrium point is stable.
Actually, we would like to remind that
h=h^{*}
is the largest positive root of the polynomial
H(h).

A stable equilibrium point means that, as the system evolves over time, the state of the system will gradually approach the stable equilibrium point and eventually stop there.
On the contrary, the system will not stop at an unstable equilibrium point and cross it.
For a given initial condition
h(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}
, due to
h=h^{*}
is a stable equilibrium point, the system (
4.7
) will move along the yellow orbit
\mathcal{H}(\frac{dh}{dt},h)=0
in Figure
1
. When it reaches the equilibrium point (the red cross in Figure
1
), the derivative becomes zero, and the system stops at the equilibrium point without crossing it.

Thus, it’s enough to consider two cases:

\displaystyle h(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq h^{*},\quad h(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}>h^{*}.

Case I:
h(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq h^{*}.
The initial state of (
4.7
) falls to the left side of the red cross. It will move to the right along the yellow orbit, and finally stop at the red cross, which implies that

h(t)=\|n_{(0,0)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\in\left[\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{16\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1})c_{1}}\right].

Case II:
h(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}>h^{*}.
The initial state of (
4.7
) falls to the right side of the red cross. It will move to the left along the yellow orbit, and finally stop at the red cross, which implies that

h(t)=\|n_{(0,0)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\in\left[\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{16\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1})c_{1}},\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\right].

In this way, we obtain the exact bound for
\|n_{(0,0)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}
in the system (
4.7
):

\displaystyle\|n_{(0,0)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}=\sup_{t\geq 0}h(t)

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},\inf_{c_{1}\in(0,2\sqrt{2})}\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{16\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1})c_{1}}\right\}.

By choosing
c_{1}=\sqrt{2}
, one can obtain

\displaystyle\|n_{(0,0)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq\max\left\{\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{32\pi^{2}}\right\}.

Through the phase plane analysis and orbit analysis in Figure
1
,
the equal sign strictly holds if and only if the system (
4.6
) becomes the system (
4.7
), otherwise,
\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}
in the system (
4.6
) is less than the system (
4.7
).

The proof is complete.
∎

Now, let us consider the full equation (
4.2
).

Lemma 4.2
.

For the equation (
4.2
), under the assumption (
2.7
), there exists a constant
A_{1}
independent of
t
and
A
, such that if
A>A_{1}
, there holds

\displaystyle\|n_{(0,0)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq\max\left\{\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\epsilon_{1},\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{32\pi^{2}}(1+\epsilon_{1})+\epsilon_{1}\right\},

where
\epsilon_{1}
can be any small positive constant satisfying
\epsilon_{1}\in(0,1)
.

Proof.

Energy estimate shows that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

(4.8)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}<\partial_{y}\big(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\big)+\partial_{y}(n_{\neq}\partial_{y}c_{\neq})_{(0,0)}+\partial_{y}\big(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)}\big)_{(0,0)},n_{(0,0)}>

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}<\partial_{y}(u_{2,\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,0)}+\partial_{y}\left(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)}\right)_{(0,0)},n_{(0,0)}>.

Direct calculation gives that

\displaystyle<\partial_{y}(n_{\neq}\partial_{y}c_{\neq})_{(0,0)},n_{(0,0)}>

\displaystyle\leq\|(n_{\neq}\partial_{y}c_{\neq})_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}

(4.9)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\epsilon_{1}}\|(n_{\neq}\partial_{y}c_{\neq})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{16}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

where
\epsilon_{1}
is a small positive constant with
\epsilon_{1}\in(0,1).

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle<\partial_{y}(u_{2,\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,0)},n_{(0,0)}>

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\epsilon_{1}}\|(u_{2,\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{16}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

(4.10)

\displaystyle<\partial_{y}\big(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)}\big)_{(0,0)},n_{(0,0)}>

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\epsilon_{1}}\|\big(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)}\big)_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{16}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle<\partial_{y}\left(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)}\right)_{(0,0)},n_{(0,0)}>

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\epsilon_{1}}\|(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{16}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

With the help of (
4.9
) and (
4.10
), as in (
4.4
) we rewrite (
4.8
) into

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}(\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}-\hat{G}(t))+\frac{\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}{A}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}A}+\frac{c_{1}}{2\sqrt{2}A}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}\|\partial_{y}n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}{4A},

where

\displaystyle\hat{G}(t)=\frac{8}{A\epsilon_{1}}\Big(

\displaystyle\|(n_{\neq}\partial_{y}c_{\neq})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|(u_{2,\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\big(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)}\big)_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\|(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big).

Similar to (
4.5
) in Lemma
4.1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}(\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}-\hat{G}(t))

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\frac{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\epsilon_{1})}{27M_{1}^{4}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{6}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}}\|n_{(0,0)}\|^{4}_{L^{2}}\Big).

(4.11)

Using Lemma
3.6
, Lemma
3.9
, Lemma
3.12
and Lemma
3.13
, under the assumption (
2.7
), if

A\geq{\rm max}\{1,C\epsilon_{1}^{-\frac{2}{3\epsilon}}(E_{1}+E_{2})^{\frac{4}{3\epsilon}}\}:=A_{1},

there holds

\displaystyle\hat{G}(t)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A\epsilon_{1}}\Big(\|\triangle c_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\|\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{y}u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}})\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A\epsilon_{1}}\Big(A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|n_{\neq}\|^{4}_{X_{a}}+A^{\frac{2}{3}}\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}(\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}})+A\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{4}_{Y_{0}}

\displaystyle+A\|\nabla u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{Y_{0}}\|u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{Y_{0}}\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}}\Big)\leq\frac{C(E_{1}^{2}+E_{2}^{2})^{2}}{A^{3\epsilon}\epsilon_{1}}\leq{\epsilon_{1}}.

Introducing the auxiliary function
g(t)=\|n_{(0,0)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}
satisfying
g\geq 0,

then there holds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}(g-\hat{G})\leq-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\frac{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\epsilon_{1})}{27M_{1}^{4}}g^{3}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}}g^{2}\Big):=G(g),\quad g\geq 0,

\displaystyle g(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

The largest positive root of
G(g)
satisfying
G(g^{*})=0
is

g^{*}=\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{16\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\epsilon_{1})c_{1}},

and the stagnation point of
G(g)
satisfying
\frac{d}{dg}G(g^{**})=0
is

g^{**}=\frac{9M_{1}^{4}}{8\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\epsilon_{1})c_{1}}.

An important fact is that, as long as
g(t)-\hat{G}\geq g^{**},

there holds
G(g)\leq G(g(t)-\hat{G}).

Thus, we need to consider two cases:

g(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq g^{*},\quad g(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\geq g^{*}.

\bullet
Case I:
\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq g^{*}.

Denote
t=t_{0}
as the first time
g(t_{0})-{\epsilon_{1}}=g^{**},
then one can construct a new system with
t\in[t_{0},T)
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}(g-\hat{G})\leq G(g)\leq-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\frac{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\epsilon_{1})}{27M_{1}^{4}}(g-\hat{G})^{3}-\frac{(g-\hat{G})^{2}}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}}\Big)=G(g-\hat{G}),

\displaystyle g(t)-\hat{G}(t)\geq 0~{\rm in}~{t\in[t_{0},T)},\quad g(t_{0})-\hat{G}(t_{0})=g^{**}+{\epsilon_{1}}-\hat{G}(t_{0}).

It should be noted that
t_{0}
is a non-negative constant and can be zero or positive infinity.

Applying the equilibrium point analysis method in Lemma
4.1
,
for
t\in[t_{0},T)
, we get

g-\hat{G}\leq g^{*}=\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{16\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\epsilon_{1})c_{1}},

and one can extend
T
to
\infty.

Combining it with
g(t)-\hat{G}\leq g^{**}+{\epsilon_{1}}<g^{*}
in
t\in[0,t_{0}],
we obtain that

g(t)\leq\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{16\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\epsilon_{1})c_{1}}+\hat{G},~{\rm for}~t\in[0,\infty).

\bullet
Case II:
\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\geq g^{*}.
Due to
G(g)\leq G(g(t)-\hat{G}),

one can construct another system:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}(g-\hat{G})\leq G(g)\leq-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\frac{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\epsilon_{1})}{27M_{1}^{4}}(g-\hat{G})^{3}-\frac{(g-\hat{G})^{2}}{2\sqrt{2}c_{1}}\Big)=G(g-\hat{G}),

\displaystyle g(0)-\hat{G}(0)=\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Applying the equilibrium point analysis method again, there holds

g(t)\in\left[\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{16\pi^{2}(2\sqrt{2}-c_{1}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\epsilon_{1})c_{1}}+\hat{G}(t),\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\hat{G}(t)\right],~{\rm for}~t\in[0,\infty).

For
\epsilon_{1}\in(0,1),
thanks to Taylor series, there holds

{2}{\Big(\sqrt{2}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{4}\epsilon_{1}\Big)^{-2}}=1+\frac{1}{2}\epsilon_{1}+\frac{3}{16}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\frac{1}{16}\epsilon_{1}^{3}+O(\epsilon_{1}^{4})\leq 1+\epsilon_{1}.

Using
\hat{G}(t)\leq{\epsilon_{1}},
we conclude that

\displaystyle\|n_{(0,0)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}=\sup_{t\geq 0}g(t)

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+{\epsilon_{1}},\frac{27M_{1}^{4}}{32\pi^{2}}(1+\epsilon_{1})+{\epsilon_{1}}\right\}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Then, we are devoted to estimate
n_{(0,\neq)}
under the assumptions (
2.7
).
By (
4.1
), the z-part non-zero mode
n_{(0,\neq)}
satisfies

\displaystyle\partial_{t}n_{(0,\neq)}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle n_{(0,\neq)}=-\frac{1}{A}\left[\nabla\cdot(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)}+\nabla\cdot(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)}\right]

(4.12)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\left[\partial_{y}\big(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)}+n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}+(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)}\big)\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\left[\partial_{z}\big(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}+n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\big)+\partial_{y}\big(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,0)}+(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)}\big)\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\left[\partial_{z}\big(u_{3,(0,0)}n_{(0,\neq)}+u_{3,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,0)}+(u_{3,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)}\big)\right].

In fact, we can regard (
4.12
) as

\displaystyle\partial_{t}n_{(0,\neq)}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle n_{(0,\neq)}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\Big(\partial_{y}\big(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)})+\partial_{z}\big(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\big)+\partial_{y}(\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}n_{(0,\neq)})

(4.13)

\displaystyle+\partial_{y}\big(n_{(0,0)}u_{2,(0,\neq)}\big)+\partial_{z}(n_{(0,0)}u_{3,(0,\neq)})\Big)+``{\rm good~terms}".

For (
4.13
), the basic energy estimate shows that

A^{2\epsilon}\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+A^{2\epsilon-1}\|\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C+C^{(1)}A^{2\epsilon-1}\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+``{\rm good~terms}".

Therefore, if we impose the condition
\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}<\frac{1}{C^{(1)}},

this energy estimate can be closed successfully, yielding the result
A^{\epsilon}\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{Y_{0}}\leq C
.

However, since
\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}
is related to
\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}
(see Lemma
4.2
), this result is not satisfactory.
A preferable outcome would be to impose a restriction on the total mass
M
without requiring a restriction on
\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}
.

Our important find is that
\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}
is enough
to control the energies
E_{1,2}
and
E_{1,3}
under the assumptions
(
2.7
).
A fact is that, for the bad nonlinear terms
(0,0)\cdot(0,\neq)
of (
4.13
), the derivative

\partial_{z}
acts only on
(0,\neq)
part, and does not affect
(0,0)
part.
That is to say, for any given functions
f
and
g
, we have

\partial_{z}^{j}(f_{(0,0)}g_{(0,\neq)})=f_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}^{j}g_{(0,\neq)},

where
j\geq 1.

Thus, for (
4.13
), we judge that
\partial_{z}
is a good derivative, while
\partial_{y}
is a bad derivative.
Due to the elliptic condition
n_{(0,\neq)}=-\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}+c_{(0,\neq)},
the bad derivative
\partial_{y}
of
n_{(0,\neq)}
can
be moved out by
c_{(0,\neq)}.

Naturally, this leads us to introduce a new idea for energy estimates.
Taking
\partial_{z}
to (
4.12
) and multiplying
\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}
on both sides,
with the help of elliptic condition
n_{(0,\neq)}=-\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}+c_{(0,\neq)}
and some precise elliptic estimates,
we can obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}}{2A^{-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\leq A^{2\epsilon}\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{C^{(2)}M(\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}})}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}+``{\rm good~terms}".

In this way, as long as we impose the condition
M<\frac{1}{C^{(2)}},

we can finish the proof by using
\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}
.
The next step is to perform precise calculations and get the constant
C^{(2)}.

Lemma 4.3
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
as long as

M<\frac{24}{5}\pi^{2},

there exists a constant
A_{2}
independent of
t
and
A
, such that if
A>A_{2}
,
there holds

A^{2\epsilon}\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\frac{\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\leq C.

Proof.

Taking
\partial_{z}
to (
4.12
) and multiplying
\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)},

by the elliptic condition
n_{(0,\neq)}=-\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}+c_{(0,\neq)},

then the energy estimates show that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\frac{\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}{2A^{-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}=

(4.14)

\displaystyle-A^{2\epsilon-1}\big(<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}\big(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>+<\partial_{z}^{2}\big(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\big),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>\big)

\displaystyle-A^{2\epsilon-1}\big(<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}(\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}n_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>+<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}\big(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,0)}\big),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>\big)

\displaystyle-A^{2\epsilon-1}\big(<\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{3,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,0)}),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>+<\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>\big)

\displaystyle-A^{2\epsilon-1}\big(<\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>+<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>\big)

\displaystyle-A^{2\epsilon-1}\big(<\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>+<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>\big)

\displaystyle-A^{2\epsilon-1}\big(<\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{3,(0,0)}n_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>+<\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{3,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>\big).

After integrating in time, we infer from (
4.14
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{2A^{-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}

(4.15)

\displaystyle\leq A^{2\epsilon}\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+T_{1,1}+\cdots+T_{1,12},

where
T_{1,1}-T_{1,5}
can be regard to bad terms and

T_{1,6}-T_{1,12}
can be regard to good terms.

Estimates of
T_{1,1}
,
T_{1,2}
and
T_{1,3}
.
First, we need some deformations

\displaystyle-<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>=<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>,

(4.16)

\displaystyle-<\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>=<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>.

(4.17)

Due to
n_{(0,\neq)}=-\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}+c_{(0,\neq)},

then

\displaystyle-<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}(\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}n_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>=<\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-<\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>+<\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>.

Using
-\partial_{y}^{2}c_{(0,0)}=n_{(0,0)}-c_{(0,0)}
and
c_{(0,0)}\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle-<\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>=\frac{<\partial_{y}^{2}c_{(0,0)},(\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>}{2},

\displaystyle-<\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}^{3}c_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>=-\frac{<\partial_{y}^{2}c_{(0,0)},(\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>}{2}\leq\frac{<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>}{2},

\displaystyle<\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>=-\frac{<\partial_{y}^{2}c_{(0,0)},(\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>}{2}\leq\frac{<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>}{2},

which imply that

\displaystyle-<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}(\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}n_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>=\frac{<\partial_{y}^{2}c_{(0,0)},(\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}-(\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}-(\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>}{2}

(4.18)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{<\partial_{y}^{2}c_{(0,0)},(\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>}{2}+\frac{<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}+(\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>}{2}.

By (
4.16
), (
4.17
) and (
4.18
), we get

\displaystyle T_{1,1}+T_{1,2}+T_{1,3}

(4.19)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>dt}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)},\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}+\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<c_{(0,0)},(\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>dt}{2A^{1-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>dt}{2A^{1-2\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<n_{(0,0)},3(\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}+(\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>dt}{2A^{1-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>dt}{2A^{1-2\epsilon}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<c_{(0,0)},(\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>dt}{2A^{1-2\epsilon}}.

By Lemma
3.3
and Young’s inequality, there hold

\displaystyle<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>

\displaystyle\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}

(4.20)

\displaystyle\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\Big(\frac{\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}{2}\Big),

\displaystyle<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>

\displaystyle\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\|\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\Big(\frac{\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}{2}\Big),

\displaystyle<n_{(0,0)},(\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>

\displaystyle\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\Big(\frac{\|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}{2}\Big).

Using Lemma
3.11
, we have

<c_{(0,0)},(\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)})^{2}>\leq\|c_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{2}.

(4.21)

By
\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}=\|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+2\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}^{3}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2},

using (
4.20
), (
4.21
) and

\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{z}^{3}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2},

we infer from (
4.19
) that

\displaystyle T_{1,1}+T_{1,2}+T_{1,3}\leq\frac{\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\Big(\frac{\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{2}-\frac{\|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{4}\Big).

Estimates of
T_{1,4}
and
T_{1,5}
.
By Lemma
3.3
, there hold

\displaystyle<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}\big(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,0)}\big),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\|\partial_{z}u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}

(4.22)

\displaystyle\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}},

and

\displaystyle<\partial_{z}^{2}\big(u_{3,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,0)}\big),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\|\partial_{z}u_{3,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}

(4.23)

\displaystyle\leq\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{1}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}u_{3,(0,\neq)}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}u_{3,(0,\neq)}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}.

It follows from (
1.5
) that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}\partial_{z}u_{2,0}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\partial_{z}u_{2,0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}P^{N_{1}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}P^{N_{3}}_{0}=\frac{\partial_{z}n_{0}}{A},\\
\partial_{t}\partial_{z}u_{3,0}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\partial_{z}u_{3,0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{2}P^{N_{1}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{2}P^{N_{2}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{2}P^{N_{3}}_{0}=0.\end{array}\right.

(4.24)

Using Lemma
3.4
and
\partial_{z}u_{3,0}=-\partial_{y}u_{2,0}
,
for
j=2,3,

there holds

\displaystyle\|\nabla(u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{j,0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq C(\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})(\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}+\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}).

(4.25)

Combining (
4.25
) with Lemma
3.14
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{j})_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}(u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{j,0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}(E_{1}^{2}+E_{2}^{2}).

(4.26)

Using above results, the
energy estimates of (
4.24
) show that

\displaystyle\frac{A^{2\epsilon}}{2}(\|\partial_{z}u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2})+A^{2\epsilon-1}\Big(\|\partial_{z}\nabla u_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

(4.27)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}}{A^{\frac{\epsilon}{2}}}\right)+A^{2\epsilon-1}\int_{0}^{t}<\partial_{z}n_{0},\partial_{z}u_{2,(0,\neq)}>dt.

Due to
\partial_{z}n_{0}=\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}=-\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}+\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)},
then

\displaystyle<\partial_{z}n_{0},\partial_{z}u_{2,(0,\neq)}>=<-\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}+\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}u_{2,(0,\neq)}>

(4.28)

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{z}\partial_{y}u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|\partial_{z}\nabla u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{2}+\frac{\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{2}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Using (
4.27
) and (
4.28
), we have

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon-1}\Big(\frac{\|\partial_{z}\nabla u_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{2}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

(4.29)

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(1+\frac{E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}}{A^{\frac{\epsilon}{2}}}\Big)+A^{2\epsilon-1}\Big(\frac{\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{2}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big).

For given positive functions
f_{j}~(j=1,2,3,4),
the following Young’s inequality holds

\displaystyle f_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}f_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}f_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}}f_{4}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{f_{1}^{2}}{4c_{1}c_{2}}+\frac{c_{2}f_{2}^{2}}{4c_{1}}+\frac{c_{1}f_{3}^{2}}{4}+\frac{c_{1}f_{4}^{2}}{4},

(4.30)

where
c_{1}
and
c_{2}
are positive constants.

For (
4.22
), we use Young’s inequality with
\{c_{1},c_{2}\}=\{\frac{1}{2},1\},
and
for (
4.23
), we use Young’s inequality with
\{c_{1},c_{2}\}=\{1,\frac{1}{2}\}.

With the help of (
4.29
),
there holds

\displaystyle T_{1,4}+T_{1,5}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\Big(\frac{\|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{2}+\frac{\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{2}+\frac{\|\partial_{z}\nabla u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{8}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{2}+\frac{\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{8}+\frac{\|\partial_{z}\nabla u_{3,(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{4}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\Big(\frac{\|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{2}+\frac{\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{2}+\frac{5\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{8}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\|\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{8}+\frac{\|c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{4}\Big)+C\Big(1+\frac{E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}}{A^{\frac{\epsilon}{2}}}\Big).

Due to

\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},~~~\|\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{z}^{3}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

when
A>A_{1},
we have

\displaystyle T_{1,4}+T_{1,5}

\displaystyle\leq C+\frac{\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\Big(\frac{\|\partial_{z}(\partial_{y}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{4}+\frac{3\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{8}

(4.31)

\displaystyle+\frac{3\|\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{4}+\frac{\|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{4}\Big)

\displaystyle=C+\frac{\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\Big(\frac{3(\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}})}{8}

\displaystyle-\frac{\|\partial_{z}(\partial_{y}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{8}+\frac{\|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{4}\Big).

Estimates of
T_{1,6}
and
T_{1,7}
.

Using Lemma
3.13
and

\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq CE_{3},

(4.32)

there holds

\displaystyle A^{\epsilon-\frac{1}{2}}\|(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\epsilon-\frac{1}{2}}\|u_{\neq}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\epsilon-\frac{1}{2}}E_{3}\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

(4.33)

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\epsilon-\frac{1}{3}}E_{3}(\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}})\leq\frac{CE_{2}E_{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\epsilon}{4}}}.

Due to
n_{\neq}=-\triangle c_{\neq}+c_{\neq},
then for
j=1,2,3,
there holds

\partial_{x}\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1}n_{\neq}=-\partial_{x}\partial_{j}c_{\neq}+\partial_{x}\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1}c_{\neq}.

The energy estimates show that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{x}\partial_{j}c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-2<\partial_{x}\partial_{j}c_{\neq},\partial_{x}\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1}c_{\neq}>+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1}c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{x}\partial_{j}c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2<\partial_{x}c_{\neq},\partial_{x}\partial_{j}^{2}\triangle^{-1}c_{\neq}>+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1}c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

which along with
\partial_{x}(\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{y}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2})\triangle^{-1}c_{\neq}=\partial_{x}c_{\neq}
imply that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\nabla\triangle^{-1}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{x}\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2<\partial_{x}c_{\neq},\partial_{x}(\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{y}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2})\triangle^{-1}c_{\neq}>+\|\partial_{x}\nabla\triangle^{-1}c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(4.34)

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{x}\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|\partial_{x}c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}\nabla\triangle^{-1}c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

By (
4.32
) and (
4.34
), we get

\displaystyle A^{\epsilon-\frac{1}{2}}\|(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\epsilon-\frac{1}{2}}E_{3}\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\frac{CE_{3}\|n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{2}E_{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon}},

(4.35)

where we use
\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{x}\nabla\triangle^{-1}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}.

Therefore, according to (
4.33
) and (
4.33
), we obtain

T_{1,6}=\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{\|(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{1}E_{2}E_{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\epsilon}{4}}},

and

T_{1,7}=\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{\|(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{1}E_{2}E_{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon}}.

Estimates of
T_{1,8}
and
T_{1,9}
.

Using Lemma
3.3
and Lemma
3.12
,
we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\leq\|\partial_{y}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\leq\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}},

which imply that

\displaystyle\quad<\partial_{z}\nabla\cdot(n_{(0,\neq)}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>\leq C\|n_{(0,\neq)}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}.

Therefore

T_{1,8}+T_{1,9}\leq\frac{C\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{1}^{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{2}\epsilon}}.

Estimates of
T_{1,10}
,
T_{1,11}
and
T_{1,12}
.

We just need to estimate
<\partial_{z}\partial_{j}(u_{j,0}n_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>,
where
j=2,3.

By (
3.27
), we get

\displaystyle<\partial_{z}\partial_{j}(u_{j,0}n_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}>

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{j,0}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|u_{2,0}\|_{H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{H^{1}})\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}},

which along with
\|f_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|f_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}}
imply that

\displaystyle T_{1,10}+T_{1,11}+T_{1,12}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{1}^{3}}{A^{\epsilon}}.

Close the energy estimates.

By above calculations, we infer from (
4.14
) and (
4.15
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}}{2A^{-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}

(4.36)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+C\left(\frac{E_{1}^{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{2}\epsilon}}+\frac{E_{1}^{2}+E_{2}^{3}+E_{3}^{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\epsilon}{4}}}+\frac{E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}+E_{3}^{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon}}\right)

\displaystyle+\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}\left[\frac{7\left(\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)}{8A^{1-2\epsilon}}-\frac{\|\partial_{z}(\partial_{y}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{8A^{1-2\epsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+C\left(\frac{E_{1}^{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{2}\epsilon}}+\frac{E_{1}^{2}+E_{2}^{3}+E_{3}^{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\epsilon}{4}}}+\frac{E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}+E_{3}^{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon}}\right)

\displaystyle+\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}\frac{5\left(\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)}{6A^{1-2\epsilon}},

where we used the fact of
\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq 3\|\partial_{z}(\partial_{y}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

The energy estimates can be closed by imposing the condition

\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}<\frac{6}{5},

which along with
\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}=\frac{M}{4\pi^{2}},

imply that

M<\frac{24\pi^{2}}{5}.

In conclusion,
when

A\geq\max\{A_{1},(E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}+E_{3}^{1})^{\frac{\epsilon}{2}},(E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}+E_{3}^{1})^{\frac{12}{4-3\epsilon}},(E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}+E_{3}^{1})^{\frac{2}{1-2\epsilon}}\}:=A_{2},

as long as
M<\frac{24\pi^{2}}{5},

there holds

\frac{A^{2\epsilon}\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{2}+\left(1-\frac{5}{6}\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}\right)\frac{\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\leq C.

We finish the proof with the help of

\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
.
∎

Lemma 4.4
.

Under conditions of Theorem
1.2
, Lemma
4.3
and the assumptions (
2.7
), there exists a constant
A_{3}
independent of
t
and
A
, such that if
A>A_{3}
, then

\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq C(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1).

Proof.

Taking
\partial_{z}^{2}
for (
4.12
), we have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{2}\triangle n_{(0,\neq)}=-\frac{1}{A}\left[\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)}+\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)}\right]

(4.37)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\left[\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}\big(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)}+n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}+(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)}\big)\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\left[\partial_{z}^{3}\big(n_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}+n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\big)+\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}\big(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,0)}+(u_{2,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)}\big)\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\left[\partial_{z}^{3}\big(u_{3,(0,0)}n_{(0,\neq)}+u_{3,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,0)}+(u_{3,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)}\big)\right].

After integrating in time, we infer from (
4.37
) that

\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}=\frac{\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{2}+T_{2,1}+T_{2,2}+...+T_{2,12}.

(4.38)

Estimate of
T_{2,1}
.

Using Lemma
3.2
, Lemma
3.6

and Lemma
3.9
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+2\|(\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\nabla\partial_{z}c_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|(n_{\neq}\nabla\partial_{z}^{2}c_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}+2\|\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\|\nabla\partial_{z}c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,z}L^{\infty}_{y}}+\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\|\nabla\partial_{z}^{2}c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,z}L^{\infty}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{1}{6}}(\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2})\leq CA^{\frac{1}{6}}E_{2}^{2}.

Thus

T_{2,1}=\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A}\leq C\frac{E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{3}}}.

Estimate of
T_{2,2}
.

For
j=2~{\rm or}~3
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,\neq}n_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+2\|(\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}n_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|(u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}+2\|\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}L^{\infty}_{y,z}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{5}{12}}(\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}})\leq CA^{\frac{5}{12}}E_{2}^{2},

which implies that

T_{2,2}=\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A}\leq C\frac{E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}}{A^{\frac{1}{12}}}.

Estimates of
T_{2,3}
and
T_{2,6}
.

Using Lemma
3.7
, Lemma
3.12

and Lemma
4.2
,
we have

\displaystyle<\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{(0,0)}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}>\leq C\|n_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}\partial_{y}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}},

which implies that

T_{2,3}+T_{2,6}=\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{(0,0)}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}),\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A}\leq\frac{CE_{1}^{2}}{A^{\frac{\epsilon}{2}}}.

Estimate of
T_{2,4}
.

Using Lemma
3.10
, Lemma
4.2

and

\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{3}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}},

we have

\displaystyle<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}),\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}>\leq\|\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}},

which implies that

T_{2,4}=\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{y}c_{(0,0)}),\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A}\leq\frac{CE_{1}^{2}}{A^{\frac{\epsilon}{2}}}.

Estimates of
T_{2,5}
and
T_{2,7}
.

Using Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
3.6
, Lemma
3.12

and Lemma
4.3
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{(0,\neq)}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+2\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\nabla\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|n_{(0,\neq)}\nabla\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|\nabla c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}+2\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\nabla\partial_{z}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}_{t,z}L^{\infty}_{y}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\partial_{z}^{2}c_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,y}L^{2}_{z}}\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}_{t,y}L^{\infty}_{z}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}},

which implies that

T_{2,5}+T_{2,7}=\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{(0,\neq)}\nabla c_{(0,\neq)})_{(0,\neq)},\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A}\leq\frac{CE_{1}^{3}}{A^{\epsilon}}.

Estimates of
T_{2,8}
and
T_{2,11}
.

It follows from (
4.24
) that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}\partial_{z}^{2}u_{2,0}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\partial_{z}^{2}u_{2,0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{2}(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}P^{N_{1}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}P^{N_{2}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}^{2}P^{N_{3}}_{0}=\frac{\partial_{z}^{2}n_{0}}{A},\\
\partial_{t}\partial_{z}^{2}u_{3,0}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\partial_{z}^{2}u_{3,0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{2}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{3}P^{N_{1}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{3}P^{N_{2}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{3}P^{N_{3}}_{0}=0.\end{array}\right.

Using (
4.25
), (
4.26
) and Lemma
4.3
, the energy estimates show that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla(u_{2,0},u_{3,0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}\leq C\Big(\frac{1+E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}}{A^{3\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{2A}\Big)\leq C\frac{E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}+1}{A^{3\epsilon}}.

(4.39)

For
j=2,3,
using Lemma
3.3
,
we have

\displaystyle<\partial_{j}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,0)}),\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}>\leq C\|n_{(0,0)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla u_{j,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}},

which along with (
4.39
) give that

\displaystyle T_{2,8}+T_{2,11}=\frac{\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{j=2}^{3}<\partial_{j}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,0)}),\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A}\leq C\frac{E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3}+1}{A^{\epsilon}}.

Estimate of
T_{2,10}
.

A direct calculation shows that

T_{2,10}=\frac{\int_{0}^{t}<u_{3,(0,0)},\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\partial_{z}^{3}n_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A}=0.

Estimates of
T_{2,9}
and
T_{2,12}
.

Thanks to
{\rm div}~u_{(0,\neq)}=0,
for
j=2,3
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}u_{3,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})\|\partial_{z}\nabla n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{-\epsilon}E_{1}\|\nabla\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{-\epsilon}E_{1}\|\nabla\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}},

which implies that

\displaystyle T_{2,8}+T_{2,11}=

\displaystyle\frac{\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{j=2}^{3}<\partial_{j}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,(0,\neq)}n_{(0,\neq)})_{\neq},\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}>dt}{A}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CE_{1}\|\nabla\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{1+\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{1}^{3}}{A^{\epsilon}}.

When

A\geq{\rm max}\{A_{2},C(1+E_{1}^{3}+E_{2}^{3})^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}\}:=A_{3},

we infer from (
4.38
) that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1).

∎

4.2.
Energy estimates for
E_{1,2}(t)
and
E_{1,3}(t)

We recall that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}u_{2,0}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle u_{2,0}+\frac{1}{A}(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}P^{N_{1}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}P^{N_{2}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}P^{N_{3}}_{0}=\frac{n_{0}}{A},\\
\partial_{t}u_{3,0}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle u_{3,0}+\frac{1}{A}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}P^{N_{1}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}P^{N_{3}}_{0}=0,\\
\partial_{y}u_{2,0}+\partial_{z}u_{3,0}=0.\end{array}\right.

(4.40)

Lemma 4.5
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
there exists a constant
A_{4}
independent of
t
and
A
, such that if
A>A_{4}
,
there holds

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}(\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}}+\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}})\leq C,

(4.41)

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}(\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}}+\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}})\leq C,

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}\|\triangle u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}}\leq C,

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}\|\min\{h_{A}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}}\leq C,

where
h_{A}(t)=A^{-\frac{2}{3}}+A^{-1}t.

Proof.

Recall that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}u_{2,0}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle u_{2,0}+\frac{1}{A}(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}P^{N_{1}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}P^{N_{2}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}P^{N_{3}}_{0}=\frac{n_{0}}{A},\\
\partial_{t}u_{3,0}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle u_{3,0}+\frac{1}{A}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}P^{N_{1}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{0}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}P^{N_{3}}_{0}=0.\end{array}\right.

(4.42)

Due to

u_{2,(0,0)}=0,

we have

<n_{0},u_{2,0}>=<n_{(0,\neq)}+n_{(0,0)},u_{2,(0,\neq)}>=<n_{(0,\neq)},u_{2,(0,\neq)}>\leq\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}},

and combining it with
<u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{2,0},u_{2,0}>+<u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0},u_{3,0}>=0
and Lemma
3.14
,
energy estimates of (
4.42
) show that

\displaystyle\frac{A^{2\epsilon}}{2}(\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2})+A^{2\epsilon-1}(\|\partial_{y}u_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{z}u_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2})

(4.43)

\displaystyle\leq C+A^{2\epsilon-1}\||u_{\neq}|^{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}(\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}})+\frac{A^{2\epsilon-1}}{2}\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C+\frac{CE_{1}E_{2}^{2}}{A^{\frac{1}{6}+\frac{\epsilon}{2}}}+\frac{A^{2\epsilon-1}}{2}\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2},

It follows from (
4.27
) and Lemma
4.3
that

A^{2\epsilon}(\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}}+\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}})\leq C(1+A^{2\epsilon-1}\|n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}})\leq C.

Estimate of
\eqref{eq:u20u30}_{2}
.

Multiplying
\triangle u_{2,0}
on
(\ref{u_zero_1})_{1}
and

\triangle u_{3,0}
on
(\ref{u_zero_1})_{2},
energy estimates give that

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}(\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}})+A^{2\epsilon-1}(\|\triangle u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}})

(4.44)

\displaystyle\leq C+CA^{2\epsilon-1}\Big(\|u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big),

where we use
A^{\epsilon}\|(u_{\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C.

By (
3.27
), we get

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon-1}\|u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{2\epsilon-1}(\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{-2\epsilon}E_{1}^{4},

(4.45)

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon-1}\|u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{2\epsilon-1}(\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{-2\epsilon}E_{1}^{4}.

Using (
4.45
), Lemma
3.14
and Lemma
4.3
,
if
A\geq\max\{A_{3},C(E_{1}^{4}+E_{2}^{4})^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}\}:=A_{4}
, we
obtain that

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}(\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}}+\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}})\leq

\displaystyle C\big(A^{-\epsilon}(E_{1}^{4}+E_{2}^{4})+1\big)\leq C.

(4.46)

Estimate of
\eqref{eq:u20u30}_{3}
.

The
H^{2}
energy estimate shows that

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}(\|\triangle u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+A^{-1}\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}})

(4.47)

\displaystyle\leq C+CA^{2\epsilon-1}\Big(\|\nabla(u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{2,0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\triangle P^{N_{3}}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big).

Due to
{\rm div}~(u\cdot\nabla u)=\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})+\partial_{y}(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})+\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3}),

thus

\displaystyle\|{\rm div}~(u\cdot\nabla u)_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(4.48)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}(u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{2,0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}(u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Using (
4.25
), (
4.48
), Lemma
3.14
and Lemma
4.3
,
there are

A^{2\epsilon-1}\|\triangle P^{N_{3}}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}=A^{2\epsilon-1}\|{\rm div}~(u\cdot\nabla u)_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{-\epsilon}(E_{1}^{4}+E_{2}^{4})\leq C,

(4.49)

and

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}\|\triangle u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}}\leq

\displaystyle C+C\big(A^{-\epsilon}(E_{1}^{4}+E_{2}^{4})+1\big)\leq C.

Estimate of
\eqref{eq:u20u30}_{4}
.

Taking
H^{2}
energy estimate on
(\ref{u_zero_1})_{2}
, then we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+A^{-1}\|\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{A}\Big(\|\nabla(u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle P^{N_{3}}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big),

which implies that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\min\{h_{A}(t),1\}\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}})+\frac{\min\{h_{A}(t),1\}}{A}\|\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

(4.50)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{A}\Big(\|\nabla(u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+h_{A}(t)\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle P^{N_{3}}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big),

where
h_{A}(t)=A^{-\frac{2}{3}}+A^{-1}t.

By (
4.25
), (
4.46
), (
4.49
), Lemma
3.14
, Lemma
4.3
and
\|h_{A}(t){\rm e}^{-aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}\leq CA^{-\frac{2}{3}},
we get

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}\|\min\{h_{A}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{Y_{0}}\leq C+\frac{C}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\int_{0}^{t}h_{A}(s)\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}ds

(4.51)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\Big(\|\nabla(u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\triangle P^{N_{3}}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C+\frac{C}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\|h_{A}(t){\rm e}^{-aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq C(1+\frac{E_{2}^{4}}{A^{\epsilon}})\leq C.

The proof is complete.
∎

Recall that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\widehat{u_{1,0}}=-\frac{1}{A}(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}})-u_{2,0},\\
\partial_{t}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\widetilde{u_{1,0}}=-\frac{1}{A}(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{u_{1,0}})-\frac{1}{A}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})_{0},\end{array}\right.

(4.52)

and

\displaystyle\widehat{u_{1,0}}|_{t=0}=0,~~~\widetilde{u_{1,0}}|_{t=0}=(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}.

Lemma 4.6
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
), if
A>A_{4}
,
there holds

A^{2\epsilon}(\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{2}})\leq C(1+A^{\frac{2}{3}}).

Proof.

Due to
{\rm div}~u_{0}=0
, we have

<u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{u_{1,0}},\widetilde{u_{1,0}}>=0.

Taking
L^{2}
product with
(\ref{u_zero_2})_{2}
, then we get

\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\|\nabla\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}{A}=-\frac{<u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{u_{1,0}},\widetilde{u_{1,0}}>}{A}-\frac{<(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})_{0},\widetilde{u_{1,0}}>}{A}.

Integrating about
t
and using Lemma
3.14
, thanks to
{\rm div}~u_{\neq}=0
, if
A>A_{4},
we have

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{Y_{0}}^{2}\leq C\Big(1+\frac{\||u_{\neq}|^{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}\Big)\leq C,

where we use
A^{2\epsilon}\|\widetilde{(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=A^{2\epsilon}\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C.

The
H^{2}
energy estimate for
(\ref{u_zero_2})_{2}
yields that

A^{2\epsilon}\|\triangle\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{Y_{0}}^{2}\leq C\Big(1+\frac{\|\nabla(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{u_{1,0}})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\Big).

(4.53)

Using Lemma
3.4
and
\partial_{z}u_{3,0}=-\partial_{y}u_{2,0}
,
if
A>A_{4},

there holds

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon-1}\|\nabla(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{u_{1,0}})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{2\epsilon-1}(\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}+\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}})\|\nabla\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-2\epsilon}E_{1}^{4}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}.

By Lemma
3.14
, we have

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon-1}\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{2\epsilon}(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}})(\|\nabla\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}})

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-\epsilon}E_{2}^{4}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}.

Therefore, it follows from (
4.53
) that

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}\|\triangle\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{Y_{0}}^{2}\leq C(1+A^{\frac{2}{3}}).

Due to
\|\nabla\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\triangle\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{2}(\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}),
we complete this proof.
∎

Lemma 4.7
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
), if
A>A_{4}
,
there holds

\displaystyle A^{2\epsilon}\Big(\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}}{A^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{4}}}{A^{3}}+\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\Big)\leq C.

(4.54)

Proof.
