Estimates of
\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}
and
\|\nabla\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{4}}
.

Taking
L^{2}
product with
(\ref{u_zero_2})_{1}
, we get

\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}=-\frac{1}{A}<u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}},\widehat{u_{1,0}}>-<u_{2,0},\widehat{u_{1,0}}>.

Thanks to
u_{2,(0,0)}=0,
there holds

\displaystyle<u_{2,0},\widehat{u_{1,0}}>=<u_{2,(0,\neq)},\widehat{u_{1,(0,\neq)}}>,

where
\widehat{u_{1,(0,\neq)}}
denotes the non-zero mode of
z
direction of
\widehat{u_{1,0}}
,
and using Hölder’s inequality, we have

<u_{2,0},\widehat{u_{1,0}}>\leq\|u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\widehat{u_{1,(0,\neq)}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}u_{2,(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Furthermore,
{\rm div}~u_{0}=0
implies that

<u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}},\widehat{u_{1,0}}>=0.

Thus, by Lemma
4.5
, we get

\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\nabla\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{\|(\widehat{u_{1,\rm in}})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}\leq C.

(4.55)

The
H^{4}
energy estimate shows that

\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{C\|\nabla\triangle(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}+\frac{C\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{1-2\epsilon}}.

(4.56)

Thanks to Lemma
3.4
, if
A>A_{4},
we have

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla\triangle(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}\leq C\frac{\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{2}}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}+\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{3}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{1}^{4}}{A^{2\epsilon}}\leq C,

(4.57)

\displaystyle\frac{\|u_{3,0}\nabla\triangle\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+\nabla u_{3,0}\triangle\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}\leq C\frac{\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{4}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{1}^{4}}{A^{2\epsilon}}\leq C,

(4.58)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\|\triangle u_{3,0}\nabla\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}\leq C\frac{\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{1}^{4}}{A^{2\epsilon}}\leq C.

(4.59)

By
\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{3}}\leq\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{4}},
there holds

\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{3}}}{A^{-1}t}\leq\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{4}}}{A^{-1}t}\leq A\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{4}},

where we use

\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}\leq\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{s}\widehat{u_{1,0}}(s)\|_{H^{2}}ds\leq t\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}.

Combining it with
\|\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{2}},

we get

\displaystyle\|\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\min\{h_{A}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big(\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{3}}}{A^{-1}t}+\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{4}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\min\{h_{A}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big(A^{2}\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{4}}\Big),

which implies that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{2\epsilon}\frac{\|\min\{h_{A}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}\Big(\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}}{A^{2}}\Big)

(4.60)

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{E_{1}^{4}}{A^{2\epsilon}}\leq C.

Using (
4.56
)-(
4.60
) and Lemma
4.5
, we have

\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}\leq C.

By
\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{4}}\leq C(\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}),

we obtain that

\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\nabla\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{4}}}{A^{3-2\epsilon}}\leq C.

(4.61)

Estimate of
\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}
.

Taking
\triangle
for
(\ref{u_zero_2})_{1}
, there holds

\partial_{t}\triangle\widehat{u_{1,0}}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}=\frac{1}{A}\triangle(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}})+\triangle u_{2,0},

which implies that

A^{2\epsilon}\|\partial_{t}\triangle\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\triangle(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}})\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}+A^{2\epsilon}\|\triangle u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}.

(4.62)

Thanks to Lemma
3.4
, if
A>A_{4},
we have

\displaystyle\frac{\|\triangle(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}})\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}\leq C\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}\leq C\frac{E_{1}^{4}}{A^{2\epsilon}}\leq C,

(4.63)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\|u_{3,0}\triangle\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+\nabla u_{3,0}\cdot\nabla\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}\leq C\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{1}^{4}}{A^{2\epsilon}}\leq C.

(4.64)

Combining
\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{3}}}{A^{-1}t}\leq A\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{4}},
with
\|\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{3}},

we get

\displaystyle\|\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\min\{h_{A}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big(\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{3}}}{A^{-1}t}+\|\triangle\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\min\{h_{A}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\big(A^{2}\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{4}}\big),

which implies that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{2\epsilon}\|\min\{h_{A}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big(\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}}{A^{2}}\Big)\leq C.

(4.65)

Using (
4.61
), (
4.62
)-(
4.65
) and Lemma
4.5
, one obtains

A^{2\epsilon}\|\partial_{t}\triangle\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{2-2\epsilon}}+C\leq C.

(4.66)

Due to
\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\widehat{u_{1,0}}=-\frac{1}{A}(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}})-u_{2,0},

using (
4.61
) and

\frac{\|u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{1-\epsilon}}\leq(\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}})\frac{\|\nabla\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{1-\epsilon}}\leq CA^{-\epsilon}E_{1}^{2},

we have

\displaystyle A^{\epsilon}\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle A^{\epsilon}\Big(\frac{\|\triangle\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A}+\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big)\leq C,

which along with (
4.66
) imply that

\displaystyle A^{\epsilon}\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\leq C.

The proof is complete.
∎

Corollary 4.1
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
according to Lemma
4.2
, Lemma
4.3
, Lemma
4.5
, Lemma
4.6
, Lemma
4.7
, when
A\geq\max\{A_{1},A_{2},A_{3},A_{4}\}:=C_{(1)},

there holds

E_{1}(t)\leq C(\|(n_{\rm in})_{(0,0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1):=E_{1}.

(4.67)

5.
Estimates for the non-zero mode: Proof of Proposition
2.2

5.1.
Energy estimates for
E_{2,1}(t)

Lemma 5.1
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
there exists a constant
A_{5}
independent of
t
and
A
, such that
if
A\geq A_{5},
there holds

E_{2,1}(t)\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+1\big).

Proof.

By (
3.1
), it is sufficient to estimate

\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}
and
\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}.

Step I: Estimate of
\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}
.
According to

(\ref{ini2})_{1}
, the non-zero mode
\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}
satisfies

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}+(y+\frac{\widehat{u_{1,0}}}{A})\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}=-\frac{1}{A}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq}-\frac{1}{A}(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq})

(5.1)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot(\partial_{x}^{2}u_{\neq}n_{0})-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}.

Applying Proposition
A.3
to (
5.1
), we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}\leq C\Big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{2,0}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(5.2)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{3,0}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle:=C\left(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+T_{3,1}+\cdots+T_{3,7}\right).

Estimate of
T_{3,1}
.
According to Lemma
4.6
, there holds
A^{\epsilon}\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}},
as long as
A
is big enough, then

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C{A^{\frac{5}{3}-2\epsilon}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2},

which implies that
\widetilde{u_{1,0}}
can be regard as a perturbation.

Estimates of
T_{3,2}
and
T_{3,3}
.
Thanks to (
3.27
) and Lemma
4.5
, for
j=2,3,
we have

\displaystyle{\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{j,0}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}\leq{\|u_{j,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}\leq CA\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}.

Estimates of
T_{3,4}
and
T_{3,5}
.
Using
\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq 2E_{3}
and
\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}+\partial_{y}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}=0
, there are

\displaystyle{\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}\leq{CE_{3}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}\leq{CAE_{3}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2,\neq},u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{X_{b}}},

and

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{3}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{3}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}.

Estimate of
T_{3,6}
.
By Lemma
3.2
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y,z}}\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}})

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{z},1)\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{z},1)\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{z},1)\partial_{x}u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{z},1)\partial_{x}u_{\neq}\|_{H^{1}}\big),

which along with velocity estimates in Lemma
3.13
imply that

\displaystyle{\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}\leq{CA^{\frac{2}{3}}(\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}})\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}}\leq{CA^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}E_{2}^{4}}.

(5.3)

Estimate of
T_{3,7}
.
According to nonlinear interaction, we have

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

(5.4)

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big).

Using Lemma
3.8
and Lemma
4.3
, it follows that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{4}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{4}}

(5.5)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla c_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{4}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla n_{\neq}\|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{5}{12}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}.

Using Lemma
3.9
and
\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq 2E_{3}
, there holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq||n_{0}||_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{3}^{2}||\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}||^{2}_{X_{b}}.

(5.6)

Similar to the proof for (
5.3
), using Lemma
3.9
, we can prove that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{4}_{X_{a}}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{2}^{4}.

(5.7)

Combining (
5.4
)-(
5.7
) give

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}(||\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}||^{2}_{X_{b}}+E_{2}^{4}+E_{3}^{2}||\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}||^{2}_{X_{b}}).

(5.8)

Using above, we infer from (
5.2
) that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}\leq C\Big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{E_{2}^{4}+(1+E_{3}^{2})\big(\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}\big)}{A^{\frac{1}{3}}}\Big).

(5.9)

Step II: Estimate of
\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}
.

The non-zero mode
\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}
satisfies

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}+\Big(y+\frac{\widehat{u_{1,0}}}{A}\Big)\partial_{x}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}-\frac{\triangle\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}}{A}=-\frac{\partial_{z}^{2}(\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}n_{\neq})}{A}-\frac{\partial_{z}^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{2\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}}{A}

(5.10)

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{z}^{2}\partial_{y}(u_{2,0}n_{\neq})}{A}-\frac{\partial_{z}^{3}(u_{3,0}n_{\neq})}{A}-\frac{\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{\neq}n_{0})}{A}-\frac{\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{\nabla\cdot\partial_{z}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}}{A}.

Applying Proposition
A.3
to (
5.10
), we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq C\Big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}

(5.11)

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{2,0}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{3,0}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{1,\neq}n_{0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{2,\neq}n_{0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{3,\neq}n_{0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{1,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{2,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{3,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}\Big).

For convenience, we rewrite (
5.11
) as

\quad\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq C(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+T_{4,1}+\cdots+T_{4,12}),

where
T_{4,1}
can be regarded as a perturbation,
T_{4,2}
and
T_{4,3}
are difficult terms.

Estimate of
T_{4,1}
:

Due to
A^{\epsilon}\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}
in Lemma
4.6
, using Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
, we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{z}^{2}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-2\epsilon}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\nabla n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{z}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-2\epsilon}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\nabla n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-2\epsilon}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

which imply that

\displaystyle\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\leq C\Big(\frac{\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}}{A^{2\epsilon}}+\frac{\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}}{A^{\frac{1}{3}+2\epsilon}}\Big).

(5.12)

Estimate of
T_{4,2}
:

Using Lemma
4.7
, it holds

\displaystyle\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}\leq\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{s}\widehat{u_{1,0}}(s)\|_{H^{2}}ds\leq CA^{-\epsilon}t.

A direct calculation shows that
\|A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t{\rm e}^{-\frac{a}{4}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}\leq C,

thus

\big\|{\rm e}^{-\frac{a}{4}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}-\epsilon}.

Combining Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
, we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{-\frac{a}{2}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}s}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}{\rm e}^{\frac{5a}{2}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}s}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\nabla n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}ds

(5.13)

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-2\epsilon}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla n_{\neq}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla n_{\neq}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{4}{3}-2\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}.

Estimate of
T_{4,3}
:

By Lemma
3.2
and setting
\alpha=1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{z}^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}(\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}),

which gives that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(5.14)

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{-\frac{a}{2}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}s}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}{\rm e}^{\frac{5a}{2}A^{-\frac{1}{3}}s}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\nabla n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}ds+C\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}A^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{4}{3}-2\epsilon}\big(\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\big).

Estimates of
T_{4,4}
and
T_{4,5}
:

For
j=2,3
, there holds

\|\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,0}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C(\|u_{j,0}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,0}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}).

By (
3.27
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{j,0}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}-2\epsilon}E_{1}^{2}\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}.

Using Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
, there hold

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\|\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,0}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

which along with (
3.27
) imply that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}-2\epsilon}E_{1}^{2}\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}},

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,0}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-2\epsilon}E_{1}^{2}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}.

Thus, we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,0}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-2\epsilon}E_{1}^{2}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}.

(5.15)

Estimate of
T_{4,6}
:

By Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
3.2
and Lemma
4.4
,
we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{1,\neq}n_{0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\big(\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(5.16)

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{2}{3}}\big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+E_{3}^{2}+1\big)\big(\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\big),

where we use
\|\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\partial_{z}n_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\leq\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}.

Estimates of
T_{4,7}
and
T_{4,8}
:

For
j=2,3
, there holds

\|\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,\neq}n_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C(\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,\neq}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}).

Due to
\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq 2E_{3},
thanks to Lemma
3.13
, thus

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,\neq}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CE_{3}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{3}^{2}(\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}).

Using Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
3.2

and
\|\partial_{z}n_{0}\|_{L^{2}}=\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}\partial_{y}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{y}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

which along with Lemma
3.13
and Lemma
4.4
give that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}\big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1\big)\big(\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\big).

Therefore, for
j=2,3
, we obtain that

\displaystyle{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,\neq}n_{0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}\leq{CA^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+E_{3}^{2}+1\big)E_{2}^{2}}.

(5.17)

Estimate of
T_{4,9}
:

Using
(\ref{appa_1})_{2}
and Lemma
3.2
,
there holds

\displaystyle\|u_{1,\neq}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

where we use
\|u_{1,\neq}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|u_{1,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,z}L^{\infty}_{y}}.

By
(\ref{appa_4})_{1}
and Lemma
3.2
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}u_{1,\neq}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}.

Lemma
3.2
show that
\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}},
thus

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{1,\neq}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}.

Therefore, we obtain that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{1,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{1,\neq}n_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

(5.18)

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2})\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}E_{2}^{4}.

Estimates of
T_{4,10}
and
T_{4,11}
:

By
(\ref{appa_1})_{2}
, Lemma
3.2
and

\|u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,z}L^{\infty}_{y}},

there is

\displaystyle\|u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

where
j=2,3.

Combining Lemma
3.13
with
\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}},
then

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{j,\neq}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}.

Using (
3.21
), Lemma
3.2

and
\|\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y,z}}\|\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}.

Thus, for
j=2,3,
we get that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{j,\neq}n_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

(5.19)

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}\big(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}\big)\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}E_{2}^{4}.

Estimate of
T_{4,12}
:

According to nonlinear interaction, there holds

\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\big(\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{0}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\big).

First, for the term of
\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{0}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}
,
using Lemma
3.2
and elliptic estimates in Lemma
3.9
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\triangle c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

(5.20)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,y}L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\triangle c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}\triangle c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

which imply that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{0}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|\partial_{z}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,y}L^{2}_{z}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}})\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

where we use
\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{z}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\leq C(\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}).

Combining it with Lemma
4.2
, Lemma
4.3
and Lemma
4.4
, we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{0}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\big)\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}.

Second, for the term of
\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}
, by Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.8
, it holds

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{1}})\leq C(\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}),

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\leq C(\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}})\leq C\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}\triangle c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Using Lemma
3.2
, we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},~~

\displaystyle\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Combining above, there is

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}})\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

which implies that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\big)\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}.

Last, for the term of
\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}
, using (
5.20
) and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},~~\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,y}L^{2}_{z}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{4}_{L^{2}},

which indicates

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{4}_{X_{a}}.

(5.21)

Thus, we conclude

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+E_{2}^{2}+1\big)E_{2}^{2}.

(5.22)

Using (
5.12
),(
5.13
)-(
5.19
),(
5.22
) and Proposition 2.1,
we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq C\Big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{(1+\|n_{\rm in}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+E_{2}^{2}+E_{3}^{2}+E_{4}^{2})E_{2}^{2}}{A^{\frac{1}{3}}}\Big).

(5.23)

Combining (
5.9
) and (
5.23
) and setting
b=a
,
when

A>\max\{A_{4},C(1+\|n_{\rm in}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+E_{2}^{2}+E_{3}^{2}+E_{4}^{2})^{3}E_{2}^{6}\}:=A_{5},

there is

E_{2,1}(t)\leq C(\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}})\leq C(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+1).

The proof is complete.
∎

5.2.
Energy estimates for
E_{2,2}(t)

Lemma 5.2
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
there exists a constant
A_{6}
independent of
t
and
A
, such that
if
A\geq A_{6},
there holds

E_{2,2}(t)\leq C.

Proof.

First, taking
\partial_{x}
to
(\ref{ini2})_{3},

one obtains a coupled system

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}\omega_{2}+y\partial_{x}^{2}\omega_{2}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\partial_{x}\omega_{2}+\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{2}=-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{x}^{2}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3}),\\
\partial_{t}\triangle u_{2}+y\partial_{x}\triangle u_{2}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle(\triangle u_{2})=\frac{1}{A}\partial_{x}^{2}n+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{2}n-\frac{1}{A}(\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2})(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})\\
\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}[\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})+\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})].\end{array}\right.

(5.24)

Applying Proposition
A.2
to (
5.24
) and setting
b=a
, we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}\leq C\Big(\|(\partial_{x}\omega_{2,{\rm in}})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\triangle u_{2,{\rm in}})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}\Big).

According to the nonlinear interaction, for
j=1,2,3,
there holds

(u\cdot\nabla u_{j})_{\neq}=u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{j,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,0}+(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,\neq})_{\neq},

Under the assumptions (
2.7
), using Proposition
2.1
, Lemma
3.14
, Lemma
3.15
and Lemma
3.16
, we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}+\frac{E_{1}^{4}+E_{2}^{4}+E_{3}^{4}+E_{5}^{4}}{A^{3\epsilon}}\Big).

(5.25)

Taking
\partial_{y}
and
\partial_{z}
to
(\ref{ini2})_{3},
there are

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{y}\omega_{2}+y\partial_{x}\partial_{y}\omega_{2}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\partial_{y}\omega_{2}+\partial_{z}\partial_{y}u_{2}+\partial_{x}\omega_{2}=-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3}),

(5.26)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{z}\omega_{2}+y\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\omega_{2}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\partial_{z}\omega_{2}+\partial_{z}^{2}u_{2}=-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}^{2}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3}).

Applying Proposition
A.1
to
(\ref{ini_4})
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\|\partial_{y}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\frac{\|(\partial_{y}\omega_{2,{\rm in}})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}

(5.27)

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\Big),

and

\displaystyle\frac{\|\partial_{z}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\frac{\|(\partial_{z}\omega_{2,{\rm in}})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}

(5.28)

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\Big).

Due to
\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}+\partial_{y}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}=0,
Lemma
3.15
is enough to estimate

\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u_{1,0}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}
.
Using Lemma
3.14
, Lemma
3.15
, Lemma
3.16
and Proposition
2.1
, we
have

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{4}{3}-3\epsilon}(1+E_{2}^{4}+E_{3}^{4}+E_{5}^{4}),

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{1-3\epsilon}(1+E_{2}^{4}+E_{3}^{4}+E_{5}^{4}).

Using (
5.25
), (
5.27
), (
5.28
) and Lemma
5.1
, when

A>\max\{A_{5},C(1+E_{2}^{2}+E_{3}^{2}+E_{5}^{2})^{\frac{4}{3\epsilon}}\}:=A_{6},

one conclude that

E_{2,2}\leq C\left(A^{\frac{3}{4}\epsilon}\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}+1+\frac{\|(n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}+1}{A^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{3}{4}\epsilon}}+\frac{1+E_{2}^{2}+E_{3}^{2}+E_{5}^{2}}{A^{\frac{3}{4}\epsilon}}\right)\leq C.

(5.29)

Finally, as long as
\epsilon\leq\frac{4}{9}
is impoesd, we have

\frac{1}{3}-\frac{3}{4}\epsilon>0,
then energy
E_{2,2}
can be closed
according to (
5.29
).
The proof is complete. ∎

Corollary 5.1
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
according to Lemma
5.1
and Lemma
5.2
, when
A\geq\max\{A_{5},A_{6}\}:=C_{(2)},

there holds

E_{2}(t)=E_{2,1}(t)+E_{2,2}(t)\leq C(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+1):=E_{2}.

(5.30)

6.
The density estimates with higher weight: Proof of Proposition
2.3

Lemma 6.1
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
there exists a constant
A_{7}
independent of
t
and
A,
such that
if
A\geq A_{7},
then

E_{4}(t)\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+1\big).

Proof.

Estimate of
\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}.

When
A\geq\max\{A_{6},C(1+E_{2}^{4}+E_{3}^{4}+E_{4}^{4}+E_{5}^{4})^{6}\}:=A_{7},
setting
b=\frac{3}{2}a,

it follows from
(\ref{n_xx_result})
that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C\Big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{E_{2}^{4}+(1+E_{3}^{2})(E_{4}^{2}+E_{5}^{2})}{A^{\frac{1}{3}}}\Big)\leq C(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1).

(6.1)

Estimate of
\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}
.

The non-zero mode
\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}
satisfies

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}+\Big(y+\frac{\widehat{u_{1,0}}}{A}\Big)\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}-\frac{\triangle\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}}{A}=-\frac{\partial_{z}(\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq})}{A}-\frac{\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{\partial_{x}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}(u_{2,0}n_{\neq})}{A}

(6.2)

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{x}\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{3,0}n_{\neq})}{A}-\frac{\nabla\cdot\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}n_{0})}{A}-\frac{\nabla\cdot\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{\nabla\cdot\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}}{A}.

Applying Proposition
A.3
to (
6.2
), we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C\Big(\|(\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}

(6.3)

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u_{2,0}\partial_{x}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u_{3,0}\partial_{x}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(n_{0}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(n_{0}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{1,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{2,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{3,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}\Big).

For convenience, we mark (
6.3
) as

\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C(\|(\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+T_{5,1}+\cdots+T_{5,11}).

Estimate of
T_{5,1}
:

Using Lemma
4.7
, there holds

\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{7}{3}-2\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2}.

Estimate of
T_{5,2}
:

Using Lemma
4.6
, there holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq CA\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{5}{3}-2\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2}.

Estimates of
T_{5,3}
and
T_{5,4}
:

Using (
3.27
) and Lemma
4.5
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{j,0}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}-2\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}.

By Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

which along with Lemma
4.5
imply that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{z}u_{j,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}-2\epsilon}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}.

Thus, we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u_{j,0}\partial_{x}n_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}-2\epsilon}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}.

Estimate of
T_{5,5}
:

Due to
{\rm div}~u_{\neq}=0,
thus

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq CE_{3}^{2}(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq CAE_{3}^{2}(\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2})\leq CA^{1-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}E_{2}^{2}E_{3}^{2}.

Estimates of
T_{5,6}
and
T_{5,7}
:

For
j=2,3
, there holds

\|\partial_{z}(n_{0}\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C(\|n_{0}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}n_{0}\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}).

Due to
\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq 2E_{3},
thanks to Lemma
3.13
, thus

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CE_{3}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CAE_{3}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}.

Using Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
3.2

and
\|\partial_{z}n_{0}\|_{L^{2}}=\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\partial_{z}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\nabla u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining above with Lemma
3.13
and Lemma
4.4
, we have

\displaystyle{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(n_{0}\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq CA\big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+E_{3}^{2}+1\big)\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{1-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+E_{3}^{2}+1\big)E_{2}^{2}.

Estimates of
T_{5,8}-T_{5,10}
:

By
(\ref{appa_1})_{2}
, Lemma
3.2
and

\|u_{\neq}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|u_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,z}L^{\infty}_{y}},

there holds

\displaystyle\|u_{\neq}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}.

Combining Lemma
3.13
with
\|n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}},
then

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{\neq}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}.

Using
(\ref{appa_2})_{1}
, (
3.21
), Lemma
3.2

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}u_{\neq}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}u_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y,z}}\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}u_{\neq}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{x}u_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y,z}}\|\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,y}L^{2}_{z}},

we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}u_{\neq}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}u_{\neq}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}.

Thus, we get that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}\big(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}\big)\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}E_{2}^{4}.

Estimate of
T_{5,11}
:

According to nonlinear interactions, we get

\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\big(\|\partial_{z}(n_{0}\partial_{x}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}(\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\nabla c_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\big).

First,
using Lemma
3.2
and Lemma
3.9
, there hold

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,y}L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}^{2}\triangle c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}\triangle c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

which imply that

\displaystyle\quad\|\partial_{z}(n_{0}\partial_{x}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|\partial_{z}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,y}L^{2}_{z}}+\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+E_{3}^{2}+1)\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\right),

where we use
\|\partial_{z}n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Using Lemma
4.4
and
\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq CE_{3}
, we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(n_{0}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\big)\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}.

Second, by Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.8
, it holds

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(\|\partial_{z}\triangle c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{1}})\leq C(\|\partial_{z}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}),

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}}\leq C(\|\partial_{z}^{2}\triangle c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}})\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Thence,

\displaystyle\quad\|\partial_{z}(\nabla c_{0}\partial_{x}n_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}\nabla c_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{z}^{2}n_{(0,\neq)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}})\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

which along with Lemma
4.2
, Lemma
4.3
, Lemma
4.4
imply that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\big(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\big)\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}.

Similar to (
5.21
),
one can prove

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq}\|^{4}_{X_{a}}.

Thus, one obtains that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{(0,\neq)}\|^{4}_{L^{2}}+1+E_{2}^{4}+E_{4}^{4}\right).

Close the energy estimate:

In this way, we have completed the estimate of
E_{4}
, when

A>\max\{A_{6},C(1+\|n_{\rm in}\|_{H^{2}}^{4}+E_{2}^{4}+E_{4}^{2}+E_{4}^{4}+E_{5}^{4})^{3}\}:=A_{7},

due to
\epsilon>\frac{1}{3},

we conclude that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1),

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C\Big(\|(\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2}}{A^{2\epsilon-\frac{2}{3}}}+\frac{1+\|n_{\rm in}\|_{H^{2}}^{4}+E_{2}^{4}+E_{3}^{4}+E_{4}^{4}+E_{5}^{4}}{A^{\frac{1}{3}}}\Big).

and

\displaystyle E_{4}(t)=\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1\big),

The proof is complete.
∎

Corollary 6.1
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
according to Lemma
6.1
, when
A\geq A_{7}:=C_{(3)},

there holds

E_{4}(t)\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1\big):=E_{4}.

(6.4)

7.
The velocity estimates with higher weight: Proof of Proposition
2.4

To estimate
\|\partial^{2}_{x}u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}
and

\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}},
it is important to
introduce the new quantity
W
defined by

W=u_{2,\neq}+\kappa u_{3,\neq},

where

V=y+\frac{\widehat{u_{1,0}}}{A},\quad{\rm and}~~\kappa=\frac{\partial_{z}V}{\partial_{y}V}.

The similar quality was first proposed by Wei-Zhang in
[
53
]
and further applied in
[
13
]
.
Here, we make a new observation: by introducing a new quasi-linear decomposition of
W,

\|\partial_{x}\nabla W\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}
and
\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}
are enough to close the estimates for
E_{5},
without relying on any additional terms.

For convenience, we denote
\mathcal{L}_{V}=\partial_{t}+V\partial_{x}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle.

For
j=2,3,
there holds

(u\cdot\nabla u_{j})_{\neq}=u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{j,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,0}+(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,\neq})_{\neq},

and we infer from (
1.3
) that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mathcal{L}_{V}u_{2,\neq}+\frac{\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}}{A}+\frac{g_{2,1}+g_{2,2}+{G_{2,3}}}{A}+\frac{\partial_{y}(P^{N_{1}}_{\neq}+P^{N_{3}}_{\neq})}{A}=\frac{n_{\neq}-\partial_{y}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}}{A},\\
\mathcal{L}_{V}u_{3,\neq}+\frac{\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}}{A}+\frac{g_{3,1}+g_{3,2}+{G_{3,3}}}{A}+\frac{\partial_{z}(P^{N_{1}}_{\neq}+P^{N_{3}}_{\neq})}{A}=\frac{-\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}}{A},\end{array}\right.

(7.1)

where

\displaystyle g_{j,1}={u_{2,0}\partial_{y}u_{j,\neq}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}},~~g_{j,2}={u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,0}},~~G_{j,3}={(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,\neq})_{\neq}}.

Due to
{\rm div}~u=0,
we have

\displaystyle{\rm div}~(u\cdot\nabla u)_{\neq}

\displaystyle=\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})_{\neq}+\partial_{y}(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})_{\neq}+\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{\neq}

\displaystyle={\rm div}~(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{\neq})_{\neq}+2(\partial_{y}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq})+2\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+2\partial_{z}g_{3,2},

which along with
\partial_{y}V=1+\frac{\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}}{A}
imply that

\displaystyle\frac{P^{N_{1}}_{\neq}+P^{N_{3}}_{\neq}}{A}=

\displaystyle-2\triangle^{-1}\left(\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\frac{{\rm div}~(u\cdot\nabla u)_{\neq}}{2A}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\triangle^{-1}\Big((1+\frac{\partial_{y}\widehat{u_{1,0}}}{A})\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\frac{\partial_{z}\widehat{u_{1,0}}}{A}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\rm div}~(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{\neq})_{\neq}}{2A}+\frac{\partial_{y}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}}{A}+\frac{\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2}}{A}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\triangle^{-1}\left(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W+\frac{\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2}}{A}+\frac{P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}}{A}\right),

where

P_{1,1}=\frac{{\rm div}~(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{\neq})_{\neq}}{2},\quad P_{1,2}=\partial_{y}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}.

Therefore, we rewrite (
7.1
) into

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}&\mathcal{L}_{V}u_{2,\neq}-2\partial_{y}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W)+\frac{\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}}{A}+\frac{g_{2,1}+g_{2,2}+{G_{2,3}}}{A}=\frac{2\partial_{y}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2})}{A}\\
&\qquad+\frac{2\partial_{y}\triangle^{-1}(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2})}{A}+\frac{n_{\neq}-\partial_{y}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}}{A},\\
&\mathcal{L}_{V}u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W)+\frac{\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}}{A}+\frac{g_{3,1}+g_{3,2}+{G_{3,3}}}{A}=\frac{2\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2})}{A}\\
&\qquad+\frac{2\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1}(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2})}{A}-\frac{\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}}{A}.\end{array}\right.

(7.2)

Then
W=u_{2,\neq}+\kappa u_{3,\neq}
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}W-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W)+\frac{\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}W}{A}+\frac{G^{(1)}+G^{(2)}}{A}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{t}\kappa-\frac{\triangle\kappa}{A})u_{3,\neq}-\frac{2\nabla\kappa\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}}{A}+\frac{n_{\neq}-\partial_{y}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{\kappa\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}}{A},

where

\displaystyle G^{(1)}=G_{2,3}+\kappa G_{3,3}-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}),

(7.3)

\displaystyle G^{(2)}=g_{2,1}+g_{2,2}+\kappa(g_{3,1}+g_{3,2})-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2}).

We introduce the following decomposition
W=W^{(1)}+W^{(2)},

satisfying

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(1)}-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W^{(1)})+\frac{\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}W}{A}=2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W^{(2)})\\
+\frac{n_{\neq}-\partial_{y}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{\kappa\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{G^{(1)}}{A},\\
\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(2)}=(\partial_{t}\kappa-\frac{\triangle\kappa}{A})u_{3,\neq}-\frac{2}{A}\nabla\kappa\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}-\frac{G^{(2)}}{A},\\
W^{(1)}_{\rm in}=W_{\rm in},~~~W^{(2)}_{\rm in}=0.\end{array}\right.

(7.4)

By Lemma
4.7
, when
A>A_{4},
there holds

A^{2\epsilon}\Big(\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}}{A^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{4}}}{A^{3}}+\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\Big)\leq C,

which implies that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\kappa\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\leq CA^{-2}\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\leq CA^{-2(1+\epsilon)},

(7.5)

\displaystyle\|\kappa\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{3}}\leq CA^{-2}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}\leq CA^{-2\epsilon}.

Lemma 7.1
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
if
A\geq A_{7},
then

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla G^{(1)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{5}{3}-\frac{7}{2}\epsilon}(E_{2}^{4}+E_{5}^{2}),

(7.6)

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}G^{(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{1-\frac{7}{2}\epsilon}E_{5}^{2},

where
\epsilon\in(\frac{1}{3},\frac{4}{9}]
is a positive constant.

Proof.

By Lemma
3.14
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
there holds

\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla G_{2,3}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{1-3\epsilon}E_{2}^{4}.

Using Lemma
3.14
and (
7.5
), we have

\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\kappa G_{2,3})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\|\kappa\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{5}{3}-5\epsilon}E_{2}^{4}.

Using Lemma
3.14
and (
7.5
) again, one obtains that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\big((\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}P_{1,1}\big)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

(7.7)

\displaystyle\leq C\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}P_{1,1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\big(\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{y}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\big)\leq CA^{1-3\epsilon}E_{2}^{4}.

Similarly, by Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
3.2
and Lemma
4.6
, we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\big((\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}P_{1,2}\big)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

(7.8)

\displaystyle\leq C\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}P_{1,2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}(u_{2,\neq},u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\nabla(u_{2,\neq},u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{5}{3}-\frac{7}{2}\epsilon}E_{5}^{2},

where we use

\|\nabla\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\nabla\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\leq\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\nabla u_{j,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Thus the proof of
\eqref{eq:g1g2}_{1}

is complete.

For
j=2,3,
by (
3.27
) and Lemma
4.5
, there holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}g_{j,1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla(u_{2,\neq},u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

(7.9)

\displaystyle\leq CA^{1-\frac{7}{2}\epsilon}E_{5}^{2}.

Due to
\partial_{y}u_{2,0}+\partial_{z}u_{3,0}=0,

we have

\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(\|\nabla\triangle u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}),

which along with Lemma
4.5
show that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}g_{j,2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{2}}+\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{1}})\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2,\neq},u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

(7.10)

\displaystyle\leq CA^{1-\frac{7}{2}\epsilon}E_{5}^{2}.

One can finish the proof of the second result by using (
7.10
) and

\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\nabla f)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}},

where
f
is selected form
g_{2,2}
and
g_{3,2}.

∎

Lemma 7.2
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
there hold

A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq\frac{CE_{5}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}+2\epsilon}},\quad A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(2)}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq\frac{CE_{5}^{2}}{A^{2\epsilon}}.

(7.11)

Proof.

Taking
\partial_{x}^{2}
to
(\ref{w_equ1})_{2},
we have

\mathcal{L}_{V}\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}=\partial_{x}^{2}\Big((\partial_{t}\kappa-\frac{\triangle\kappa}{A})u_{3,\neq}\Big)-\frac{2}{A}\partial_{x}^{2}\Big(\nabla\kappa\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}\Big)-\frac{\partial_{x}^{2}G^{(2)}}{A}.

Applying Proposition
A.3
, there is

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\Big(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(\partial_{t}\kappa u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\big(\frac{{\triangle\kappa}}{A^{2}}u_{3,\neq}\big)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

(7.12)

\displaystyle+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\big(\frac{\nabla\kappa}{A^{2}}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}\big)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)+\frac{C\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}G^{(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}.

Using Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
, we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}(\partial_{t}\kappa u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{t}\kappa\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\leq C\|\partial_{t}\kappa\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}({\triangle\kappa}u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\triangle\kappa\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\leq C\|\kappa\|^{2}_{H^{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}(\nabla\kappa\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\nabla\kappa\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla u_{3,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\kappa\|^{2}_{H^{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla u_{3,\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

which along with (
7.5
) imply that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(\partial_{t}\kappa u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\big(\frac{{\triangle\kappa}}{A^{2}}u_{3,\neq}\big)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

(7.13)

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|\partial_{t}\kappa\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+A^{-2}\|\kappa\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{3}}\big)\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{-\frac{4}{3}-2\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\big(\frac{\nabla\kappa}{A^{2}}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}\big)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\frac{\|\kappa\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{3}}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}\leq\frac{C\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2}}{A^{1+2\epsilon}}.

(7.14)

Using (
7.13
), (
7.14
) and Lemma
7.1
, we infer from (
7.12
) that

\displaystyle A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq\frac{CA^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}+2\epsilon}}+\frac{CA^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|e^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}G^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\leq\frac{CE_{5}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}+2\epsilon}}.

(7.15)

For
j=2,3,
there holds

\mathcal{L}_{V}\partial_{x}\partial_{j}W^{(2)}=\partial_{x}\partial_{j}\Big((\partial_{t}\kappa-\frac{\triangle\kappa}{A})u_{3,\neq}\Big)-\frac{2\partial_{x}\partial_{j}(\nabla\kappa\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla u_{3,\neq})}{A}-\partial_{j}V\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}-\frac{\partial_{x}\partial_{j}G^{(2)}}{A}.

Applying Proposition
A.3
and setting
b=\frac{3}{2}a
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\partial_{j}W^{(2)}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}

\displaystyle\leq CA\Big(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(\partial_{t}\kappa u_{3,\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\big(\frac{{\triangle\kappa}}{A^{2}}u_{3,\neq}\big)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\big(\frac{\nabla\kappa}{A^{2}}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}\big)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle+C\Big(A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{j}V\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}G^{(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}\Big).

Due to

\|\nabla V\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C(1+\frac{\|\nabla\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}}{A})\leq C(1+\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3}}}{A})\leq C,

(7.16)

using (
7.13
), (
7.14
), (
7.15
) and Lemma
7.1
, the proof of (
7.11
) is complete.

∎

Lemma 7.3
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
), there exists a constant
A_{8}
independent of
t
and
A,
such that
if
A\geq A_{8},
then

A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1\big).

Proof.

Applying Proposition
A.4
to
(\ref{w_equ1})_{1}
, we get

\displaystyle\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\|\triangle W_{\rm in}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(n_{\neq}-\partial_{y}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\kappa\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}

(7.17)

\displaystyle+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\triangle\big((\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W^{(2)})\big)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla G^{(1)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|e^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}W)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}\Big).

Due to
\triangle P^{N_{2}}=\partial_{y}n,

then

\triangle(n_{\neq}-\partial_{y}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq})=(\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2})n_{\neq},

which implies that

\|\nabla(n_{\neq}-\partial_{y}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{x}\partial_{z})n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

and

\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(n_{\neq}-\partial_{y}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\right).

(7.18)

Due to (
7.5
) and

\|\partial_{z}\nabla P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

therefore

\displaystyle\|\nabla(\kappa\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\kappa\|^{2}_{H^{3}}\|\partial_{z}\nabla P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq CA^{-2\epsilon}\|\partial_{z}\nabla P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq CA^{-2\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

which implies that

\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\kappa\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}-2\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}.

(7.19)

By (
7.5
), (
7.16
) and

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{y},\partial_{z})\nabla V\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|\triangle V\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq CA^{-1}\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}\leq CA^{-\epsilon},

(7.20)

there holds

A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\triangle\big((\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W^{(2)})\big)\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(2)}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2}.

(7.21)

Recalling
W=u_{2,\neq}+\kappa u_{3,\neq},
then using Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
3.2
, Lemma
4.6
and (
7.5
), we get

\displaystyle\|e^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}W)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(7.22)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\nabla\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,z}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\|e^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(u_{2,\neq}+\kappa u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{t,x,z}}^{2}+\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|e^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}(u_{2,\neq}+\kappa u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\|e^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}(u_{2,\neq},u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{5}{3}-\frac{7}{2}\epsilon}E_{5}^{2}.

By (
7.17
), (
7.18
), (
7.19
), (
7.21
), (
7.22
), Lemma
6.1
, Lemma
7.1
and Lemma
7.2
, as long as

A>\max\{A_{7},E_{5}^{\frac{3}{3\epsilon-1}}\}:=A_{8},

we conclude that

\displaystyle A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|u_{\rm in}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\frac{\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}+\frac{E_{2}^{4}+E_{5}^{2}}{A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}+\frac{E_{5}^{2}}{A^{2\epsilon-\frac{2}{3}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|u_{\rm in}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1\big).

Here, to close the energy estimate successfully,
\epsilon
must satisfy

\epsilon\in(\frac{1}{3},\frac{4}{9}]
.
∎

Corollary 7.1
.

According to Lemma
7.2
and Lemma
7.3
,
when
A>A_{8},

there holds

\displaystyle A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}\nabla W\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}\nabla(W^{(1)},W^{(2)})\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C\big(\|(n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+1\big).

Lemma 7.4
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
if
A\geq A_{8},
then

A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1\big).

Proof.

For
\eqref{ini_6}_{2}
, taking
\partial_{x}^{2}
and applying Proposition

A.3
, by (
7.16
), we get

\displaystyle A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\Bigg(\|(u_{3,\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}W\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(g_{2,2},g_{3,1},g_{3,2})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(P_{1,1},P_{1,2},\partial_{x}G_{3,3})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|e^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\Bigg).

Using Corollary
7.1
, one obtains

A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}W\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}\nabla W\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C\big(\|(n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+1\big).

By (
7.7
), (
7.8
), (
7.9
), (
7.10
)
and Lemma
3.14
, when
A>A_{8},

there holds
