\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(g_{2,2},g_{3,1},g_{3,2})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}\\
+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(P_{1,1},P_{1,2},\partial_{x}G_{3,3})\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{1-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}\leq C\frac{E_{5}^{2}+E_{2}^{4}}{A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}\leq C.

Due to
\triangle P^{N_{2}}=\partial_{y}n,
then

\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{z}P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}\leq\frac{\|{\rm e}^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{4}^{2}}{A^{\frac{4}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}\leq C.

Using Lemma
4.6
, when
A>A_{8},
we have

\frac{\|e^{\frac{3}{2}aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CA\|\widetilde{u_{1,0}}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{5}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}\leq\frac{CE_{5}^{2}}{A^{2\epsilon}}\leq C.

Combining above, we conclude that

A^{\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1\big).

∎

So far, due to
W=u_{2,\neq}+\kappa u_{3,\neq}
, we can complete the energy estimation of
E_{5}
with

E_{5}(t)=A^{\frac{3}{4}\epsilon}\left(\|\partial^{2}_{x}u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\right)\leq CA^{\frac{3}{4}\epsilon}\left(\|\partial_{x}\nabla W\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{\frac{3}{2}a}}\right).

Corollary 7.2
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.2
and the assumptions (
2.7
),
according to Corollary
7.1
and Lemma
7.4
, when
A\geq A_{8}:=C_{(4)},

there holds

E_{5}(t)\leq C\big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+1\big):=E_{5}.

(7.23)

Appendix A
Space-time estimates and Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequalities

First, the following one-dimensional Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequalities are frequently used, which can be found in
[
42
]
and
[
35
]
.

Lemma A.1
.

For
\tau\in\mathbb{R}
, there hold

\displaystyle\|h(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|h(\tau)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\prime}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

(A.1)

\displaystyle\|h(\tau)\|_{L^{2}}\leq\left(\frac{16\pi^{2}}{27}\right)^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|h(\tau)\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|h^{\prime}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}.

(A.2)

Next, we recall the space-time estimate of the following equation (see Proposition 4.1 in
[
53
]
).
Let

\displaystyle\partial_{t}f-\frac{1}{A}\triangle f+y\partial_{x}f=\partial_{x}f_{1}+f_{2}+\nabla\cdot f_{3},\quad t\in[0,T]

(A.3)

where
f_{1}
,
f_{2}
and
f_{3}
are given functions.

Proposition A.1
.

Let
f
be a solution of (
A.3
) and
P_{0}f=P_{0}f_{1}=P_{0}f_{2}=P_{0}f_{3}=0,
then there holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A^{\frac{1}{3}}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla f_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\triangle^{-1}\partial_{x}f_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|f_{\rm in,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla f_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right),

where “a” is a given non-negative constant.

To estimate the coupled terms
(\triangle u_{2},\partial_{x}\omega_{2}),
we have
the following proposition, which will be proved later.

Proposition A.2
.

Assume that
(h_{1},h_{2})
satisfy

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}h_{1}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle h_{1}+y\partial_{x}h_{1}=\nabla\cdot g_{1},\\
\\
\partial_{t}h_{2}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle h_{2}+y\partial_{x}h_{2}+\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1}h_{1}=\nabla\cdot g_{2},\end{array}\right.

for
t\in[0,T],
where
h_{1},h_{2},g_{1}
and
g_{2}
are given functions and
P_{0}h_{1}=P_{0}h_{2}=P_{0}g_{1}=P_{0}g_{2}=0.
Then for
b\geq 0,
it holds that

\|h_{1,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}+\|h_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}\leq C\left(\|(h_{1,\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(h_{2,\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}g_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}g_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right).

We also need the following proposition (see Proposition 4.4 in
[
53
]
).

Proposition A.3
.

Assume that
f
satisfies

\partial_{t}f-\frac{1}{A}\triangle f+\left(y+\frac{\widehat{u_{1,0}}}{A}\right)\partial_{x}f=\partial_{x}f_{1}+f_{2}+\nabla\cdot f_{3},

for
t\in[0,T],
where
f
,
f_{1}
,
f_{2}
and
f_{3}
are given functions and
P_{0}f=P_{0}f_{1}=P_{0}f_{2}=P_{0}f_{3}=0
.
As long as

\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{4}}}{A}+\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}<c,

for some small
c
independent of
A
and
T,
then for
b\geq 0
there holds

\displaystyle\|f_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}\leq C\left(\|(f_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla f_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right).

The following proposition can be derived from Proposition 4.9 in
[
53
]
by the same way, and we omitted it.

Proposition A.4
.

Assume that
f
satisfies

\partial_{t}f-\frac{1}{A}\triangle f+\left(y+\frac{\widehat{u_{1,0}}}{A}\right)\partial_{x}f-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}f)=f_{1}+f_{2},

for
t\in[0,T],
where
f,f_{1}
and
f_{2}
are given functions and
P_{0}f=P_{0}f_{1}=P_{0}f_{2}=0.

As long as

\frac{\|\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{4}}}{A}+\|\partial_{t}\widehat{u_{1,0}}\|_{H^{2}}<c,

for some small
c
independent of
A
and
T,
then for
b\geq 0,
there holds

\displaystyle\|\triangle f_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{b}}\leq C\left(\|f_{\rm in,\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla f_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\triangle f_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right).

Proof of Proposition
A.2
.

We follow the same route as in
[
53
]
.
By using new variables
(\tilde{x},\tilde{y},\tilde{z})=(x-ty,y,z)
, let
\tilde{h}_{k,\neq}(\tilde{x},\tilde{y},\tilde{z})=h_{k,\neq}(x,y,z)

and
\tilde{g}_{k}(\tilde{x},\tilde{y},\tilde{z})=g_{k}(x,y,z),
where
k=1,2
.
After dropping the superscript, we get

\displaystyle\partial_{t}h_{1}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle_{L}h_{1}=\nabla_{L}\cdot g_{1},

\displaystyle\partial_{t}h_{2}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle_{L}h_{2}=-\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\triangle_{L}^{-1}h_{1}+\nabla_{L}\cdot g_{2},

where
\nabla_{L}=(\partial_{x},\partial_{y}-t\partial_{x},\partial_{z}).

Doing the Fourier transform, we get

\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}\widehat{h_{1}}+\frac{r(t)}{A}\widehat{h_{1}}=i(k_{1},k_{2}-tk_{1},k_{3})\cdot\widehat{g}_{1},\\
\partial_{t}\widehat{h_{2}}+\frac{r(t)}{A}\widehat{h_{2}}=-k_{1}k_{3}r(t)^{-1}\widehat{h_{1}}+i(k_{1},k_{2}-tk_{1},k_{3})\cdot\widehat{g_{2}},\end{array}

(A.4)

where
r(t)={k_{1}^{2}}+\left(k_{2}-tk_{1}\right)^{2}+{k_{3}^{2}}.

First, we study the following equation:

\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}\widehat{f}+\frac{1}{A}r(t)\widehat{f}={ik_{1}}\widehat{f}^{1}+\widehat{f}^{2}.\end{array}

(A.5)

The solution of (
A.5
) is given by

\displaystyle\widehat{f}(t)={\rm e}^{-\frac{1}{A}r_{1}(t)}\widehat{f}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{-\frac{1}{A}(r_{1}(t)-r_{1}(s))}\left(ik_{1}\widehat{f}^{1}(s)+\widehat{f}^{2}(s)\right)ds:=F_{(0)}+F_{(1)}+F_{(2)},

where

r_{1}(t)=\int_{0}^{t}\left(k_{1}^{2}+\left(k_{2}-sk_{1}\right)^{2}+{k_{3}^{2}}\right)ds.

For
t_{1}>t_{2}
, there holds

\displaystyle{r_{1}(t_{1})-r_{1}(t_{2})}

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{2}}^{t_{1}}\left(k_{1}^{2}+\left(k_{2}-sk_{1}\right)^{2}+k_{3}^{2}\right)ds\geq\frac{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{3}k_{1}^{2}}{12}.

Thus, for
k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z}
and
k_{1}\neq 0
, we have

{A}^{-1}\left(r_{1}(t)-r_{1}(s)\right)\geq{A}^{-1}\frac{(t-s)^{3}k_{1}^{2}}{12}\geq(b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(t-s)-C,

(A.6)

where
b
is a positive constant.
Thanks to (
A.6
), thus

|F_{(0)}|=|{\rm e}^{-{A}^{-1}r_{1}(t)}\widehat{f}(0)|\leq C{\rm e}^{-(b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}|\widehat{f}(0)|,

which implies that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F_{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}\leq C|\widehat{f}(0)|,~~~

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F_{(0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}|\widehat{f}(0)|^{2}.

(A.7)

Using H
\rm\ddot{o}
lder’s inequality, we obtain that

\displaystyle|F_{(1)}|

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{(b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(s-t)}|k_{1}\widehat{f}^{1}(s)|ds

(A.8)

\displaystyle\leq C\|k_{1}r(s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(0,t)}\|{\rm e}^{(b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(s-t)}r(s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{1}(s)\|_{L^{2}(0,t)}

\displaystyle\leq C\|{\rm e}^{(b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(s-t)}r(s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{1}(s)\|_{L^{2}(0,t)}|k_{1}|^{\frac{1}{2}}{(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{-\frac{1}{4}}},

where we use

\|k_{1}r(s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}=\int_{0}^{T}\frac{k_{1}^{2}}{k_{1}^{2}+(k_{2}-sk_{1})^{2}+k_{3}^{2}}ds\leq\frac{|k_{1}|\pi}{(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Using (
A.8
),
we have

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F_{(1)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}\leq C{|k_{1}|(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}r(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{1}(t)\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2},

(A.9)

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F_{(1)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}\leq C{|k_{1}|(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{T}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}s+bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}(s-t)}r(s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{1}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,t)}dt

\displaystyle\leq C{|k_{1}|(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{s}^{T}{\rm e}^{2bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}s+2bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}(s-t)}r(s)|\widehat{f}^{1}(s)|^{2}dtds

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{\frac{1}{3}}t}r(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{1}(t)\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2}{|k_{1}|(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}}.

Using integration by parts and (
A.6
), we get

\displaystyle\qquad\|{\rm e}^{-{A}^{-1}\left(r_{1}(t)-r_{1}(s)\right)}{\rm e}^{(b+\frac{1}{2})A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(t-s)}r(s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(0,t)}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{-2{A}^{-1}\left(r_{1}(t)-r_{1}(s)\right)}{\rm e}^{(2b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(t-s)}r(s)ds=\frac{A}{2}\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{(2b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(t-s)}d{\rm e}^{-(\frac{2}{A}\left(r_{1}(t)-r_{1}(s)\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{A}{2}{\rm e}^{(2b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(t-s)-2A^{-1}\left(r_{1}(t)-r_{1}(s)\right)}|_{s=0}^{s=t}-\frac{A}{2}\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{-2A^{-1}\left(r_{1}(t)-r_{1}(s)\right)}d{\rm e}^{(2b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(t-s)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A}{2}+\frac{CA}{2}(2b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-2(b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(t-s)}e^{(2b+1)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(t-s)}ds\leq CA,

then we have

\displaystyle|F_{(2)}|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{-A^{-1}\left(r_{1}(t)-r_{1}(s)\right)}|\widehat{f}^{2}(s)|ds=\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{-A^{-1}\left(r_{1}(t)-r_{1}(s)\right)}{\rm e}^{(b+\frac{1}{2})A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(t-s)}{\rm e}^{(b+\frac{1}{2})A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(s-t)}|\widehat{f}^{2}(s)|ds

\displaystyle\leq\|{\rm e}^{-A^{-1}\left(r_{1}(t)-r_{1}(s)\right)}{\rm e}^{(b+\frac{1}{2})A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(t-s)}r(s)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(0,t)}\|{\rm e}^{(b+\frac{1}{2})A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(s-t)}r(s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{2}(s)\|_{L^{2}(0,t)}

\displaystyle\leq CA^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\rm e}^{(b+\frac{1}{2})A^{-\frac{1}{3}}(s-t)}r(s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{2}(s)\|_{L^{2}(0,t)}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}(s-t)}r(s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{2}(s)\|_{L^{2}(0,t)},

which implies that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F_{(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{2}(t)\|_{L^{2}(0,T)},

(A.10)

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F_{(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}\leq CA^{\frac{4}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{2}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}.

Combining (
A.7
), (
A.9
), (
A.10
)
and

\displaystyle\|k_{1}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|k_{1}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C|k_{1}|(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}^{2},

(A.11)

we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}^{2}+A^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2}+\|k_{1}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2}

(A.12)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(|\widehat{f}(0)|^{2}+\frac{|k_{1}|}{(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}r(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{1}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{2}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}\right).

Due to

\partial_{t}\left({\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\right)+A^{-1}r(t){\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}={ik_{1}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}^{1}+{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}^{2}+bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f},

multiplying the complex conjugate of
{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}
and using
\rm H\ddot{o}
lder’s inequality, one obtains

\displaystyle A^{-1}\|r(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(|\widehat{f}(0)|^{2}+A^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}+\|r(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}^{1}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}\|{k_{1}}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}

\displaystyle+\|r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}^{2}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}\|e^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}r(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}\Big),

which along with (
A.11
) and (
A.12
) give that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{f}\|^{2}_{Z_{b}}\leq C\Big(|\widehat{f}(0)|^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}r(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{1}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}{|k_{1}|(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{f}^{2}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}\Big),

(A.13)

where

\|\widehat{f}\|^{2}_{Z_{b}}=\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}^{2}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2}}{A^{\frac{1}{3}}}+\|k_{1}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2}+\frac{\|r(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{f}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}}{A}.

Then, for (
A.4
), we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{h_{1}}\|^{2}_{Z_{b}}\leq C(|\widehat{h_{1}}(0)|^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{g_{1}}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}),

(A.14)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{h_{2}}\|^{2}_{Z_{b}}\leq C(|\widehat{h_{2}}(0)|^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{g_{2}}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}+\frac{|k_{1}|\|k_{3}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{h}_{1}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2}}{(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}).

Due to
\int_{0}^{T}\frac{|k_{1}|}{k_{1}^{2}+(k_{2}-sk_{1})^{2}+k_{3}^{2}}ds\leq\frac{\pi}{(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}},
there holds

\displaystyle\qquad|k_{1}|(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|k_{3}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{h}_{1}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{h}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}^{2}|k_{1}|(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|k_{3}r(t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(0,T)}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{h}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}^{2}\frac{k_{3}^{2}}{(k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{T}\frac{|k_{1}|}{k_{1}^{2}+(k_{2}-sk_{1})^{2}+k_{3}^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{h}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}^{2}\frac{k_{3}^{2}}{k_{1}^{2}+k_{3}^{2}}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{h}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}^{2},

which along with (
A.14
) imply that

\|\widehat{h_{1}}\|^{2}_{Z_{b}}+\|\widehat{h_{2}}\|^{2}_{Z_{b}}\leq C\Big(|\widehat{h_{1}}(0)|^{2}+|\widehat{h_{2}}(0)|^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{g_{1}}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\widehat{g_{2}}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,T)}\Big).

(A.15)

For (
A.15
), with the help of Plancherel’s theorem, integrating
k_{2}
in
\mathbb{R}
and summing up in

k_{1},k_{3}\in\mathbb{Z}
with
k_{1}\neq 0,
we complete the proof.
∎

Appendix B
Local well-posedness and blow-up criterion

In this section, we aim to establish the local well-posedness results of strong solutions to the system (
1.5
), which state as follows.

Theorem B.1
.

Assume that the non-negative initial data
n_{\rm in}(x,y,z)\in H^{2}\cap L^{1}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T})

and
u_{\rm in}(x,y,z)\in H^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T})
.
Then there exist a positive constant
T^{*}=T^{*}(\|n_{\rm in}\|_{H^{2}\cap L^{1}},\|u_{\rm in}\|_{H^{2}})
and a unique strong solution
(n,u)\in C([0,T^{*});H^{2}(\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}))
for the system of
(
1.5
)
, with the initial data
(n_{\rm in},u_{\rm in})
. Moreover, if the strong solution of
(
1.5
)
can be uniquely continued to a maximal existence
time
T^{*}
, then

\displaystyle\lim_{t\nearrow T^{*}}A^{-\frac{1}{12}}\|(\nabla u)(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}}+\|n(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}=+\infty.

(B.1)

Proof.

We sketch the proof and it is divided into four parts.

Step I. A priori estimates.
By Cauchy-Lipschitz theorem for the standard approximation system (for example, see Sec 3.5 in
[
6
]
), it is enough to close the energy estimates for the terms of the higher order derivatives. For
A>1
, claim that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{A}\left(\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

(B.2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{8}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|n\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{C}{A}\|n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{10}+C\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

It follows from the first equation of (
1.5
) that

\partial_{t}\partial_{ij}n+\partial_{ij}(y\partial_{x}n)+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{ij}(u\cdot\nabla n)-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{ij}\triangle n=-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{ij}\nabla\cdot(n\nabla c),

with
i,j=1,2,3.
Multiplying it by
\partial_{ij}n
and integrating over
\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(B.3)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{ij}(u\cdot\nabla n)\partial_{ij}n-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{ij}\nabla\cdot(n\nabla c)\partial_{ij}n+C\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle B_{1}+B_{2}+C\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

By integration by parts, Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality and
\nabla\cdot u=0,
we have

\displaystyle B_{1}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{ij}u\cdot\nabla n\partial_{ij}n-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}u\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n\partial_{ij}n-\frac{2}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{j}u\cdot\nabla\partial_{i}n\partial_{ij}n

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}n\partial_{ij}u\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n+\frac{2}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{i}n\partial_{j}u\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4A}\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|n\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{3}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4A}\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A}\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2A}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{8}.

For
B_{2},
note that similar estimates as in Lemma
3.9
imply that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{2}\|\nabla^{j+k}c(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\nabla^{k}n(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\quad k=0,1,2.

(B.4)

By (
B.4
) and Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality we get

\displaystyle B_{2}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{ij}(n\nabla c)\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{ij}n\nabla c\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n+\frac{2}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{i}n\partial_{j}\nabla c\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n+\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}n\partial_{ij}\nabla c\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A}\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\triangle c\|_{L^{3}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|n\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{ij}\nabla c\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A}\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|n\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{C}{A}\|n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Collecting the estimates of
B_{1}-B_{2},
we get by
(\ref{eq B})
that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(B.5)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{8}+C\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|n\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{C}{A}\|n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Rewrite the third equation of (
1.5
) as follows

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{ij}u+\partial_{ij}(y\partial_{x}u)+\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\partial_{ij}u_{2}\\
0\\
0\\
\end{array}\right)-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{ij}\triangle u+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{ij}(u\cdot\nabla u)+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{ij}\nabla P=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
\frac{\partial_{ij}n}{A}\\
0\\
\end{array}\right),

with
i,j=1,2,3.
Multiplying it by
\partial_{ij}u
and integrating
over
\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(B.6)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{ij}u_{2}\partial_{ij}u_{1}-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{ij}(u\cdot\nabla u)\partial_{ij}u

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{ij}n\partial_{ij}u_{2}+C\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}:=C_{1}+C_{2}+C_{3}+C\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For
C_{1}
and
C_{3},
direct calculations indicate that

C_{1}\leq\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\quad C_{3}\leq\frac{1}{2A}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2A}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

The estimate of
C_{2}
is similar as
B_{1},
and we arrive

C_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2A}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{10}.

Collecting
C_{1}-C_{3},
then (
B.6
) yields that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{3}{2A}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{10}+\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(B.7)

Combining (
B.5
) with (
B.7
), the proof of (
B.2
) is complete.

Step II. Uniqueness.

Let
\tilde{n}=n^{1}-n^{2},\tilde{c}=c^{1}-c^{2}
and
\tilde{u}=u^{1}-u^{2},
then direct calculation shows that
(\tilde{n},\tilde{c},\tilde{u})
satisfies

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}\tilde{n}+y\partial_{x}\tilde{n}+\frac{1}{A}\tilde{u}\cdot\nabla n^{1}+\frac{1}{A}u^{2}\cdot\nabla\tilde{n}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\tilde{n}=-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot(\tilde{n}\nabla c^{1})-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot(n^{2}\nabla\tilde{c}),\\
\triangle\tilde{c}+\tilde{n}-\tilde{c}=0,\\
\partial_{t}\tilde{u}+y\partial_{x}\tilde{u}+\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\tilde{u}_{2}\\
0\\
0\\
\end{array}\right)-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\tilde{u}+\frac{1}{A}\tilde{u}\cdot\nabla u^{1}+\frac{1}{A}u^{2}\cdot\nabla\tilde{u}+\frac{1}{A}\nabla\tilde{P}=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
\frac{\tilde{n}}{A}\\
0\\
\end{array}\right).\end{array}\right.

(B.8)

As
\nabla\cdot u^{2}=\nabla\cdot\tilde{u}=0,
the basic energy estimates give

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}n^{1}\tilde{u}\cdot\nabla\tilde{n}

(B.9)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\tilde{n}\nabla c^{1}\cdot\nabla\tilde{n}+\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}n^{2}\nabla\tilde{c}\cdot\nabla\tilde{n}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

For
I_{1},
using Sobolev inequality, we get

I_{1}\leq\frac{1}{A}\|n^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4A}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|n^{1}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For
I_{2},
using (
B.4
) we have

\|\nabla c^{1}\|_{L^{6}}\leq C\|\triangle c^{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|n^{1}\|_{L^{2}},

(B.10)

and by Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla c^{1}\|_{L^{6}}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|n^{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4A}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|n^{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Similarly,
I_{3}
is bounded by

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{A}\|n^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\tilde{c}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|n^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4A}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|n^{2}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Collecting
I_{1}-I_{3},
then (
B.9
) yields that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{4A}\|\nabla\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{C}{A}\left(\|n^{1}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|n^{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{4}+\|n^{2}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\right)\left(\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(B.11)

Using
\nabla\cdot u^{2}=\nabla\cdot\tilde{u}=0
again, then multiplying
(\ref{eq:tilde})_{3}
by
\tilde{u}
, the energy estimates give

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\tilde{u}_{2}\tilde{u}_{1}+\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}u^{1}\tilde{u}\cdot\nabla\tilde{u}

(B.12)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\tilde{n}\tilde{u}_{2}:=J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}.

For
J_{1}
and
J_{3},
it holds that

J_{1}\leq\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\quad J_{3}\leq\frac{1}{2A}\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2A}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For
J_{2},
using Sobolev inequality, we have

J_{2}\leq\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|u^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4A}\|\nabla\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|u^{1}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

From the estimates of
J_{1}-J_{3},
we get by
(\ref{tilde u})
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{3}{4A}\|\nabla\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(\frac{1}{A}\|u^{1}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+1\right)\left(\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(B.13)

Combining (
B.11
) with (
B.13
), we get

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\frac{1}{A}\|n^{1}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|n^{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{4}+\frac{1}{A}\|n^{2}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|u^{1}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+1\right)\left(\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\eta(t)\left(\|\tilde{n}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

Notice that
\eta\in L^{1}([0,T^{*}))
due to
(n^{1},u^{1})
and
(n^{2},u^{2})
are strong solutions. Then Gronwall’s inequality implies
(\tilde{n}_{\rm in},\tilde{u}_{\rm in})=(0,0),
and
\tilde{c}=0
due to (
B.4
) .
Thus, the uniqueness result is established.

Step III. The positivity of
n
.

Setting
n_{+}=\max\{0,n\},-n_{-}=\min\{0,n\},
then
n=n_{+}-n_{-}.
Multiplying the first equation of (
1.5
) by
n_{-},
we arrive

\displaystyle\partial_{t}n_{-}n_{-}+y\partial_{x}n_{-}n_{-}+\frac{1}{A}u\cdot\nabla n_{-}n_{-}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle n_{-}n_{-}=-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot(n\nabla c)n_{-}.

Then the energy estimates give

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|n_{-}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla n_{-}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}n_{-}^{2}\triangle c

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A}\|n_{-}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(\|c\|_{H^{2}}+\|n\|_{H^{2}}),

which follows that

\|n_{-}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|n_{-}(0,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}e^{\int_{0}^{t}\frac{C}{A}(\|c\|_{H^{2}}+\|n\|_{H^{2}})ds}.

Note that
n_{-}(0,\cdot)=0
due to
n(0,\cdot)\geq 0,
which means
n_{-}=0.
Thus
n=n_{+}\geq 0.

Step IV. Blow-up criterion.

Assume that (
B.1
) fails. That is

\displaystyle\sup_{0<t<T^{*}}A^{-\frac{1}{12}}\|(\nabla u)(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{x}^{2},\partial_{z}^{2})n(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}}+\|n(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C.

(B.14)

For
0<t<T^{*}
, claim that

\partial_{t}\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|\partial_{y}\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\right),

(B.15)

and

\partial_{t}\left(\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{A}\left(\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\left(\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\right),

(B.16)

which implies that the strong solution can be extended to time
T^{*}
.

Note that
\partial_{y}n
satisfies

\partial_{t}\partial_{y}n+\partial_{x}n+y\partial_{y}\partial_{x}n+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}(u\cdot\nabla n)-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\triangle n=-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\nabla\cdot(n\nabla c),

then the energy estimates give

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|\partial_{y}\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{x}n\partial_{y}n-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{y}(u\cdot\nabla n)\partial_{y}n

(B.17)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}\partial_{y}\nabla\cdot(n\nabla c)\partial_{y}n:=K_{1}+K_{2}+K_{3}.

For
K_{1},
using (
B.14
), we have

K_{1}\leq C\|\partial_{x}^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\left(\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\right).

Using
\nabla\cdot u=0
, integration by parts and (
B.14
), we can estimate
K_{2}
by

\displaystyle K_{2}=\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}}n\partial_{y}u\cdot\partial_{y}\nabla n\leq\frac{1}{2A}\|\partial_{y}\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|n\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2A}\|\partial_{y}\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C.

Recall that
\|n\|_{L^{1}}=\|n_{\rm in}\|_{L^{1}}\leq C.
Then
due to elliptic estimates (
B.4
), Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality and (
B.14
), there holds

\displaystyle K_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4A}\|\partial_{y}\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|n\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}\nabla c\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4A}\|\partial_{y}\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|n\|_{L^{\infty}}\|n\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A}\|\partial_{y}\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Collecting
K_{1}-K_{3},
we get by
(\ref{n'})
that

\partial_{t}\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|\partial_{y}\nabla n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|\partial_{y}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\right),

which gives (
B.15
).

Moreover, recall (
B.2
), and by using (
B.14
) and (
B.15
), we get

\partial_{t}\left(\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{A}\left(\|\nabla^{3}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\left(\|\nabla^{2}n\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\right),

which gives (
B.16
).

To sum up, the proof is complete.
∎
